=== FILE: deploy/oracle_setup.sh ===
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

APP_USER="${SUDO_USER:-$USER}"
SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
APP_DIR="$(cd "${SCRIPT_DIR}/.." && pwd)"
APP_PORT="${APP_PORT:-8080}"
PULL_MODELS="${PULL_MODELS:-false}"

echo "=== ORACLE_CHAT Production Setup ==="
echo "User:      ${APP_USER}"
echo "Directory: ${APP_DIR}"
echo "Port:      ${APP_PORT}"

echo "[1/6] Installing Python, Git, zstd, and firewall tools..."
sudo apt-get update -y
sudo DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive apt-get install -y \
    python3 python3-venv python3-pip git curl zstd ufw iptables-persistent

echo "[2/6] Setting up Python virtual environment and dependencies..."
cd "${APP_DIR}"
if [ ! -d "${APP_DIR}/venv" ]; then
    python3 -m venv "${APP_DIR}/venv"
fi
"${APP_DIR}/venv/bin/pip" install --upgrade pip
"${APP_DIR}/venv/bin/pip" install -r "${APP_DIR}/requirements.txt"

echo "[3/6] Ensuring .env configuration exists..."
if [ ! -f "${APP_DIR}/.env" ]; then
    cp "${APP_DIR}/.env.example" "${APP_DIR}/.env"
    echo "Created .env from .env.example — remember to add your OPENROUTER_API_KEY or set LLM_PROVIDER=ollama."
fi

echo "[4/6] Installing and enabling Ollama..."
if ! command -v ollama >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    curl -fsSL https://ollama.com/install.sh | sh
fi
sudo systemctl enable ollama
sudo systemctl restart ollama

if [ "${PULL_MODELS}" = "true" ]; then
    echo "Pulling gemma3:4b (this may take a few minutes)..."
    ollama pull gemma3:4b
fi

echo "[5/6] Configuring OS firewall (iptables + ufw) for SSH (22) and App (${APP_PORT})..."
sudo ufw allow OpenSSH >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
sudo ufw allow "${APP_PORT}/tcp" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
sudo iptables -C INPUT -p tcp --dport "${APP_PORT}" -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null || \
    sudo iptables -I INPUT 6 -m state --state NEW -p tcp --dport "${APP_PORT}" -j ACCEPT
sudo netfilter-persistent save >/dev/null 2>&1 || true

echo "[6/6] Installing and starting systemd service (oracle-chat.service)..."
TMP_UNIT="/tmp/oracle-chat.service"
sed \
    -e "s|__APP_USER__|${APP_USER}|g" \
    -e "s|__APP_DIR__|${APP_DIR}|g" \
    -e "s|__APP_PORT__|${APP_PORT}|g" \
    "${APP_DIR}/deploy/oracle-chat.service" > "${TMP_UNIT}"

sudo cp "${TMP_UNIT}" /etc/systemd/system/oracle-chat.service
rm -f "${TMP_UNIT}"
sudo systemctl daemon-reload
sudo systemctl enable oracle-chat
sudo systemctl restart oracle-chat

echo "=== Setup Complete ==="
echo "Check service status: sudo systemctl status oracle-chat"
echo "Follow live logs:     sudo journalctl -u oracle-chat -f"
echo "Test locally:         curl -s http://localhost:${APP_PORT}/health"

=== END FILE ===

=== FILE: frontend/static/js/a2ui_renderer.js ===
(function () {
    const ICON_MAP = {
        check: "\u2713",
        close: "\u2715",
        refresh: "\u21bb",
        star: "\u2605",
        info: "\u2139",
        warning: "\u26a0",
        error: "\u26d4",
        search: "\uD83D\uDD0D",
        home: "\uD83C\uDFE0",
        settings: "\u2699",
        mail: "\u2709",
        phone: "\u260E",
        calendar: "\uD83D\uDCC5",
        clock: "\u23F0",
        user: "\uD83D\uDC64",
        add: "+",
        delete: "\uD83D\uDDD1",
        edit: "\u270E",
        send: "\u27A4",
    };

    function isSafeMediaUrl(url) {
        if (!url || typeof url !== "string") {
            return false;
        }
        const trimmed = url.trim();
        if (trimmed.startsWith("/uploads/") || trimmed.startsWith("/static/")) {
            return true;
        }
        try {
            const parsed = new URL(trimmed, window.location.origin);
            return parsed.protocol === "https:" || parsed.protocol === "http:";
        } catch (err) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    function decodePointerSegment(seg) {
        return seg.replace(/~1/g, "/").replace(/~0/g, "~");
    }

    function getByPointer(dataModel, pointer) {
        if (!pointer || pointer === "/") {
            return dataModel;
        }
        const cleaned = pointer.startsWith("/") ? pointer.slice(1) : pointer;
        const segments = cleaned.split("/").map(decodePointerSegment);
        let current = dataModel;
        for (const seg of segments) {
            if (current === null || current === undefined) {
                return undefined;
            }
            current = current[seg];
        }
        return current;
    }

    function setByPointer(dataModel, pointer, value) {
        if (!pointer || pointer === "/") {
            if (value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)) {
                Object.assign(dataModel, value);
            }
            return;
        }
        const cleaned = pointer.startsWith("/") ? pointer.slice(1) : pointer;
        const segments = cleaned.split("/").map(decodePointerSegment);
        let current = dataModel;
        for (let i = 0; i < segments.length - 1; i += 1) {
            const key = segments[i];
            if (!current[key] || typeof current[key] !== "object") {
                current[key] = {};
            }
            current = current[key];
        }
        const lastKey = segments[segments.length - 1];
        if (value === null) {
            delete current[lastKey];
        } else {
            current[lastKey] = value;
        }
    }

    function getBindingPath(prop) {
        if (!prop || typeof prop !== "object") {
            return null;
        }
        if (typeof prop["@path"] === "string") {
            return prop["@path"];
        }
        if (typeof prop.path === "string") {
            return prop.path;
        }
        return null;
    }

    function resolveValue(surface, prop, fallback = undefined) {
        if (prop === undefined || prop === null) {
            return fallback;
        }
        if (typeof prop !== "object" || Array.isArray(prop)) {
            return prop;
        }
        const bindingPath = getBindingPath(prop);
        if (bindingPath) {
            const bound = getByPointer(surface.dataModel, bindingPath);
            if (bound !== undefined) {
                return bound;
            }
        }
        if ("literalString" in prop) return prop.literalString;
        if ("literalNumber" in prop) return prop.literalNumber;
        if ("literalBoolean" in prop) return prop.literalBoolean;
        if ("literalArray" in prop) return prop.literalArray;
        return fallback !== undefined ? fallback : prop;
    }

    function resolveContextObject(surface, rawContext) {
        if (!rawContext || typeof rawContext !== "object") {
            return {};
        }
        const resolved = {};
        for (const [key, val] of Object.entries(rawContext)) {
            resolved[key] = resolveValue(surface, val);
        }
        return resolved;
    }

    function applyJustifyAndAlign(el, comp) {
        const justifyMap = {
            start: "flex-start",
            center: "center",
            end: "flex-end",
            spaceBetween: "space-between",
            spaceAround: "space-around",
            spaceEvenly: "space-evenly",
        };
        const alignMap = {
            start: "flex-start",
            center: "center",
            end: "flex-end",
            stretch: "stretch",
        };
        if (comp.justify && justifyMap[comp.justify]) {
            el.style.justifyContent = justifyMap[comp.justify];
        }
        if (comp.align && alignMap[comp.align]) {
            el.style.alignItems = alignMap[comp.align];
        }
    }

    function renderComponent(surface, componentId, onAction, visited = new Set()) {
        if (!componentId || visited.has(componentId)) {
            return document.createTextNode("");
        }
        const comp = surface.components.get(componentId);
        if (!comp) {
            return document.createTextNode("");
        }

        const nextVisited = new Set(visited);
        nextVisited.add(componentId);

        const type = comp.component;

        switch (type) {
            case "Card": {
                const card = document.createElement("div");
                card.className = "a2ui-card";
                if (comp.child) {
                    card.appendChild(renderComponent(surface, comp.child, onAction, nextVisited));
                } else if (Array.isArray(comp.children)) {
                    for (const childId of comp.children) {
                        card.appendChild(renderComponent(surface, childId, onAction, nextVisited));
                    }
                }
                return card;
            }

            case "Row": {
                const row = document.createElement("div");
                row.className = "a2ui-row";
                applyJustifyAndAlign(row, comp);
                const children = Array.isArray(comp.children) ? comp.children : [];
                for (const childId of children) {
                    row.appendChild(renderComponent(surface, childId, onAction, nextVisited));
                }
                return row;
            }

            case "Column": {
                const col = document.createElement("div");
                col.className = "a2ui-column";
                applyJustifyAndAlign(col, comp);
                const children = Array.isArray(comp.children) ? comp.children : [];
                for (const childId of children) {
                    col.appendChild(renderComponent(surface, childId, onAction, nextVisited));
                }
                return col;
            }

            case "List": {
                const list = document.createElement("div");
                list.className = comp.direction === "horizontal" ? "a2ui-list horizontal" : "a2ui-list";
                applyJustifyAndAlign(list, comp);
                const children = Array.isArray(comp.children) ? comp.children : [];
                for (const childId of children) {
                    list.appendChild(renderComponent(surface, childId, onAction, nextVisited));
                }
                return list;
            }

            case "Divider": {
                const hr = document.createElement("hr");
                hr.className = comp.axis === "vertical" ? "a2ui-divider vertical" : "a2ui-divider";
                return hr;
            }

            case "Tabs": {
                const wrapper = document.createElement("div");
                wrapper.className = "a2ui-tabs";
                const items = Array.isArray(comp.tabs)
                    ? comp.tabs
                    : Array.isArray(comp.tabItems)
                      ? comp.tabItems
                      : [];
                if (items.length === 0) {
                    return wrapper;
                }

                const header = document.createElement("div");
                header.className = "a2ui-tabs-header";
                const panel = document.createElement("div");
                panel.className = "a2ui-tabs-panel";

                let activeIdx = 0;
                const renderTabBody = () => {
                    panel.innerHTML = "";
                    const currentTab = items[activeIdx];
                    if (currentTab && currentTab.child) {
                        panel.appendChild(renderComponent(surface, currentTab.child, onAction, nextVisited));
                    }
                    Array.from(header.children).forEach((btn, idx) => {
                        btn.classList.toggle("active", idx === activeIdx);
                    });
                };

                items.forEach((item, idx) => {
                    const btn = document.createElement("button");
                    btn.type = "button";
                    btn.className = idx === 0 ? "a2ui-tab-btn active" : "a2ui-tab-btn";
                    btn.textContent = String(resolveValue(surface, item.title, `Tab ${idx + 1}`));
                    btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
                        activeIdx = idx;
                        renderTabBody();
                    });
                    header.appendChild(btn);
                });

                wrapper.appendChild(header);
                wrapper.appendChild(panel);
                renderTabBody();
                return wrapper;
            }

            case "Modal": {
                const container = document.createElement("div");
                container.className = "a2ui-modal-wrapper";
                const triggerId = comp.trigger || comp.entryPointChild;
                const contentId = comp.content || comp.contentChild;

                const triggerEl = renderComponent(surface, triggerId, () => {
                    const backdrop = document.createElement("div");
                    backdrop.className = "a2ui-modal-backdrop";

                    const dialog = document.createElement("div");
                    dialog.className = "a2ui-modal-dialog";
                    dialog.appendChild(renderComponent(surface, contentId, onAction, nextVisited));

                    const footer = document.createElement("div");
                    footer.className = "a2ui-modal-footer";
                    const closeBtn = document.createElement("button");
                    closeBtn.type = "button";
                    closeBtn.className = "a2ui-btn";
                    closeBtn.textContent = "Close";
                    closeBtn.addEventListener("click", () => backdrop.remove());
                    footer.appendChild(closeBtn);

                    dialog.appendChild(footer);
                    backdrop.appendChild(dialog);
                    backdrop.addEventListener("click", (ev) => {
                        if (ev.target === backdrop) {
                            backdrop.remove();
                        }
                    });
                    document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
                }, nextVisited);

                container.appendChild(triggerEl);
                return container;
            }

            case "Text": {
                const textEl = document.createElement("div");
                const variant = comp.variant || comp.usageHint || "body";
                textEl.className = `a2ui-text variant-${variant}`;
                textEl.textContent = String(resolveValue(surface, comp.text, ""));
                return textEl;
            }

            case "Icon": {
                const iconEl = document.createElement("span");
                iconEl.className = "a2ui-icon";
                const iconName = String(resolveValue(surface, comp.name, "info")).toLowerCase();
                iconEl.textContent = ICON_MAP[iconName] || "\u25CF";
                iconEl.title = iconName;
                return iconEl;
            }

            case "Image": {
                const url = String(resolveValue(surface, comp.url, ""));
                if (!isSafeMediaUrl(url)) {
                    const fallback = document.createElement("div");
                    fallback.className = "a2ui-text variant-caption";
                    fallback.textContent = "[Blocked or invalid image URL]";
                    return fallback;
                }
                const img = document.createElement("img");
                img.className = "a2ui-image";
                img.src = url;
                img.alt = String(resolveValue(surface, comp.alt, "A2UI Image"));
                if (comp.fit) {
                    img.style.objectFit = comp.fit === "scaleDown" ? "scale-down" : comp.fit;
                }
                return img;
            }

            case "Video": {
                const url = String(resolveValue(surface, comp.url, ""));
                if (!isSafeMediaUrl(url)) {
                    const fallback = document.createElement("div");
                    fallback.className = "a2ui-text variant-caption";
                    fallback.textContent = "[Blocked or invalid video URL]";
                    return fallback;
                }
                const video = document.createElement("video");
                video.className = "a2ui-video";
                video.controls = true;
                video.preload = "metadata";
                video.src = url;
                if (comp.posterUrl && isSafeMediaUrl(String(comp.posterUrl))) {
                    video.poster = String(comp.posterUrl);
                }
                return video;
            }

            case "AudioPlayer": {
                const url = String(resolveValue(surface, comp.url, ""));
                const box = document.createElement("div");
                box.className = "a2ui-audio-box";
                const desc = resolveValue(surface, comp.description, "");
                if (desc) {
                    const label = document.createElement("small");
                    label.textContent = String(desc);
                    box.appendChild(label);
                }
                if (!isSafeMediaUrl(url)) {
                    const fallback = document.createElement("span");
                    fallback.className = "a2ui-text variant-caption";
                    fallback.textContent = "[Blocked or invalid audio URL]";
                    box.appendChild(fallback);
                    return box;
                }
                const audio = document.createElement("audio");
                audio.className = "a2ui-audio";
                audio.controls = true;
                audio.preload = "metadata";
                audio.src = url;
                box.appendChild(audio);
                return box;
            }

            case "Button": {
                const btn = document.createElement("button");
                btn.type = "button";
                const variant = comp.variant || "default";
                btn.className = `a2ui-btn variant-${variant}`;
                if (comp.child) {
                    btn.appendChild(renderComponent(surface, comp.child, onAction, nextVisited));
                } else {
                    btn.textContent = String(resolveValue(surface, comp.label || comp.text, "Button"));
                }
                btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
                    const rawAction = comp.action || {};
                    const evt = rawAction.event || rawAction;
                    const actionName = evt.name || "button_click";
                    const context = resolveContextObject(surface, evt.context || {});
                    onAction({
                        name: actionName,
                        surface_id: surface.surfaceId,
                        source_component_id: componentId,
                        context,
                    });
                });
                return btn;
            }

            case "TextField": {
                const field = document.createElement("div");
                field.className = "a2ui-field";
                const labelText = resolveValue(surface, comp.label, "");
                if (labelText) {
                    const label = document.createElement("label");
                    label.className = "a2ui-field-label";
                    label.textContent = String(labelText);
                    field.appendChild(label);
                }
                const variant = comp.variant || "shortText";
                const bindingPath = getBindingPath(comp.value);
                const initialVal = String(resolveValue(surface, comp.value, ""));
                const input = document.createElement(variant === "longText" ? "textarea" : "input");
                if (variant === "longText") {
                    input.className = "a2ui-textarea";
                    input.rows = 3;
                } else {
                    input.className = "a2ui-input";
                    input.type =
                        variant === "obscured"
                            ? "password"
                            : variant === "number"
                              ? "number"
                              : "text";
                }
                input.value = initialVal;
                if (comp.placeholder) {
                    input.placeholder = String(resolveValue(surface, comp.placeholder, ""));
                }
                input.addEventListener("input", () => {
                    if (bindingPath) {
                        const val = variant === "number" && input.value !== "" ? Number(input.value) : input.value;
                        setByPointer(surface.dataModel, bindingPath, val);
                    }
                });
                field.appendChild(input);
                return field;
            }

            case "CheckBox": {
                const label = document.createElement("label");
                label.className = "a2ui-checkbox-label";
                const checkbox = document.createElement("input");
                checkbox.type = "checkbox";
                const bindingPath = getBindingPath(comp.value);
                checkbox.checked = Boolean(resolveValue(surface, comp.value, false));
                checkbox.addEventListener("change", () => {
                    if (bindingPath) {
                        setByPointer(surface.dataModel, bindingPath, checkbox.checked);
                    }
                });
                const span = document.createElement("span");
                span.textContent = String(resolveValue(surface, comp.label, "Option"));
                label.appendChild(checkbox);
                label.appendChild(span);
                return label;
            }

            case "ChoicePicker": {
                const field = document.createElement("div");
                field.className = "a2ui-field";
                const labelText = resolveValue(surface, comp.label, "");
                if (labelText) {
                    const label = document.createElement("label");
                    label.className = "a2ui-field-label";
                    label.textContent = String(labelText);
                    field.appendChild(label);
                }

                const options = Array.isArray(comp.options) ? comp.options : [];
                const isMulti = comp.variant === "multipleSelection";
                const displayStyle = comp.displayStyle || "checkbox";
                const bindingPath = getBindingPath(comp.value);
                let currentVal = resolveValue(surface, comp.value, isMulti ? [] : []);
                let selectedSet = new Set(
                    Array.isArray(currentVal)
                        ? currentVal.map(String)
                        : currentVal
                          ? [String(currentVal)]
                          : []
                );

                const optionsContainer = document.createElement("div");
                optionsContainer.className =
                    displayStyle === "chips" ? "a2ui-choice-options" : "a2ui-choice-options vertical";

                const syncSelection = () => {
                    if (bindingPath) {
                        setByPointer(surface.dataModel, bindingPath, Array.from(selectedSet));
                    }
                    Array.from(optionsContainer.children).forEach((chipEl) => {
                        const val = chipEl.dataset.value;
                        chipEl.classList.toggle("selected", selectedSet.has(val));
                    });
                };

                options.forEach((opt) => {
                    const optValue = String(opt.value ?? opt.label ?? "");
                    const optLabel = String(resolveValue(surface, opt.label, optValue));

                    const itemLabel = document.createElement("label");
                    itemLabel.className = "a2ui-choice-chip";
                    itemLabel.dataset.value = optValue;

                    const input = document.createElement("input");
                    input.type = isMulti ? "checkbox" : "radio";
                    input.name = `choice_${surface.surfaceId}_${componentId}`;
                    input.value = optValue;
                    input.checked = selectedSet.has(optValue);

                    input.addEventListener("change", () => {
                        if (isMulti) {
                            if (input.checked) {
                                selectedSet.add(optValue);
                            } else {
                                selectedSet.delete(optValue);
                            }
                        } else {
                            selectedSet = new Set([optValue]);
                        }
                        syncSelection();
                    });

                    const span = document.createElement("span");
                    span.textContent = optLabel;

                    itemLabel.appendChild(input);
                    itemLabel.appendChild(span);
                    optionsContainer.appendChild(itemLabel);
                });

                syncSelection();
                field.appendChild(optionsContainer);
                return field;
            }

            case "Slider": {
                const field = document.createElement("div");
                field.className = "a2ui-field";
                const labelText = resolveValue(surface, comp.label, "");
                if (labelText) {
                    const label = document.createElement("label");
                    label.className = "a2ui-field-label";
                    label.textContent = String(labelText);
                    field.appendChild(label);
                }

                const sliderRow = document.createElement("div");
                sliderRow.className = "a2ui-slider-row";

                const range = document.createElement("input");
                range.type = "range";
                range.min = String(comp.min ?? 0);
                range.max = String(comp.max ?? 100);
                if (comp.steps) {
                    const span = Number(range.max) - Number(range.min);
                    if (span > 0) {
                        range.step = String(span / Number(comp.steps));
                    }
                }

                const bindingPath = getBindingPath(comp.value);
                const startVal = Number(resolveValue(surface, comp.value, range.min));
                range.value = String(startVal);

                const readout = document.createElement("span");
                readout.className = "a2ui-slider-value";
                readout.textContent = range.value;

                range.addEventListener("input", () => {
                    readout.textContent = range.value;
                    if (bindingPath) {
                        setByPointer(surface.dataModel, bindingPath, Number(range.value));
                    }
                });

                sliderRow.appendChild(range);
                sliderRow.appendChild(readout);
                field.appendChild(sliderRow);
                return field;
            }

            case "DateTimeInput": {
                const field = document.createElement("div");
                field.className = "a2ui-field";
                const labelText = resolveValue(surface, comp.label, "");
                if (labelText) {
                    const label = document.createElement("label");
                    label.className = "a2ui-field-label";
                    label.textContent = String(labelText);
                    field.appendChild(label);
                }

                const enableDate = comp.enableDate !== false;
                const enableTime = Boolean(comp.enableTime);
                const input = document.createElement("input");
                input.className = "a2ui-input";

                if (enableDate && enableTime) {
                    input.type = "datetime-local";
                } else if (enableTime) {
                    input.type = "time";
                } else {
                    input.type = "date";
                }

                const bindingPath = getBindingPath(comp.value);
                const rawVal = String(resolveValue(surface, comp.value, ""));
                if (rawVal) {
                    if (input.type === "datetime-local") {
                        input.value = rawVal.slice(0, 16);
                    } else if (input.type === "date") {
                        input.value = rawVal.slice(0, 10);
                    } else {
                        input.value = rawVal.slice(0, 5);
                    }
                }

                input.addEventListener("change", () => {
                    if (bindingPath) {
                        setByPointer(surface.dataModel, bindingPath, input.value);
                    }
                });

                field.appendChild(input);
                return field;
            }

            default: {
                const unknown = document.createElement("div");
                unknown.className = "a2ui-text variant-caption";
                unknown.textContent = `[Unsupported A2UI component: ${type}]`;
                return unknown;
            }
        }
    }

    function renderA2uiMessages(messages, onAction) {
        const container = document.createElement("div");
        container.className = "a2ui-surfaces-container";
        const surfaces = new Map();

        const getOrCreateSurface = (surfaceId) => {
            if (!surfaces.has(surfaceId)) {
                surfaces.set(surfaceId, {
                    surfaceId,
                    rootId: "root",
                    components: new Map(),
                    dataModel: {},
                });
            }
            return surfaces.get(surfaceId);
        };

        for (const msg of messages || []) {
            if (!msg || typeof msg !== "object") continue;

            if (msg.createSurface) {
                const cs = msg.createSurface;
                const s = getOrCreateSurface(cs.surfaceId || "main");
                if (cs.root) s.rootId = cs.root;
                if (cs.dataModel && typeof cs.dataModel === "object") {
                    Object.assign(s.dataModel, cs.dataModel);
                }
                if (Array.isArray(cs.components)) {
                    for (const comp of cs.components) {
                        if (comp && comp.id) s.components.set(comp.id, comp);
                    }
                }
            }

            if (msg.updateDataModel) {
                const udm = msg.updateDataModel;
                const s = getOrCreateSurface(udm.surfaceId || "main");
                const val = "value" in udm ? udm.value : udm.contents;
                setByPointer(s.dataModel, udm.path || "/", val);
            }

            if (msg.updateComponents) {
                const uc = msg.updateComponents;
                const s = getOrCreateSurface(uc.surfaceId || "main");
                const comps = Array.isArray(uc.components) ? uc.components : [];
                for (const comp of comps) {
                    if (comp && comp.id) s.components.set(comp.id, comp);
                }
            }

            if (msg.deleteSurface && msg.deleteSurface.surfaceId) {
                surfaces.delete(msg.deleteSurface.surfaceId);
            }
        }

        for (const surface of surfaces.values()) {
            const surfaceEl = document.createElement("div");
            surfaceEl.className = "a2ui-surface";
            const rootId = surface.components.has(surface.rootId)
                ? surface.rootId
                : surface.components.keys().next().value;
            if (rootId) {
                surfaceEl.appendChild(renderComponent(surface, rootId, onAction));
            }
            container.appendChild(surfaceEl);
        }

        return container;
    }

    window.OracleA2uiRenderer = {
        renderA2uiMessages,
    };
})();

=== END FILE ===

const ollamaCustomModelInput = document.getElementById("ollama-custom-model");
const resetSettingsBtn = document.getElementById("reset-settings-btn");
const settingsSaveFeedback = document.getElementById("settings-save-feedback");
const openLogsFromSettingsBtn = document.getElementById("open-logs-from-settings-btn");

// DOM Elements - Log Window
const logsToggleBtn = document.getElementById("logs-toggle-btn");
const logErrorBadge = document.getElementById("log-error-badge");
const logDrawer = document.getElementById("log-drawer");
const logOutput = document.getElementById("log-output");
const copyLogsBtn = document.getElementById("copy-logs-btn");
const clearLogsBtn = document.getElementById("clear-logs-btn");
const closeLogsBtn = document.getElementById("close-logs-btn");
const logFilterButtons = document.querySelectorAll(".filter-btn");

let currentSettings = loadSettings();
let currentSessionId = loadOrCreateSessionId();
const logEntries = [];
let activeLogFilter = "all";
let isSending = false;
let isUploading = false;
let pendingAttachment = null;

// ---------------------------
// Logging
// ---------------------------
function formatTimestamp(date = new Date()) {
    return date.toTimeString().split(" ")[0];
}

function addLog(level, source, message, details = null) {
    const entry = {
        timestamp: formatTimestamp(),
        level,
        source,
        message,
        details: details ? (typeof details === "string" ? details : JSON.stringify(details)) : "",
    };
    logEntries.push(entry);
    renderLogs();
    updateErrorBadge();
}

function updateErrorBadge() {
    const errorCount = logEntries.filter((entry) => entry.level === "ERROR").length;
    if (errorCount > 0) {
        logErrorBadge.textContent = String(errorCount);
        logErrorBadge.classList.remove("hidden");
    } else {
        logErrorBadge.classList.add("hidden");
    }
}

function formatLogLine(entry) {
    const suffix = entry.details ? ` | ${entry.details}` : "";
    return `[${entry.timestamp}] [${entry.level}] [${entry.source}] ${entry.message}${suffix}`;
}

function renderLogs() {
    const filtered = logEntries.filter((entry) => {
        if (activeLogFilter === "error") return entry.level === "ERROR";
        if (activeLogFilter === "event") return entry.level !== "ERROR";
        return true;
    });

    logOutput.innerHTML = "";
    if (filtered.length === 0) {
        const empty = document.createElement("div");
        empty.className = "log-entry level-info";
        empty.textContent = "No matching log entries yet.";
        logOutput.appendChild(empty);
        return;
    }

    for (const entry of filtered) {
        const line = document.createElement("div");
        line.className = `log-entry level-${entry.level.toLowerCase()}`;
        line.textContent = formatLogLine(entry);
        logOutput.appendChild(line);
    }
    logOutput.scrollTop = logOutput.scrollHeight;
}

function toggleLogDrawer(forceOpen = null) {
    const shouldOpen = forceOpen !== null ? forceOpen : logDrawer.classList.contains("hidden");
    logDrawer.classList.toggle("hidden", !shouldOpen);
    if (shouldOpen) renderLogs();
}

async function copyLogsToClipboard() {
    const text = logEntries.map(formatLogLine).join("\n") || "No log entries.";
    try {
        if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
            await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
        } else {
            const temp = document.createElement("textarea");
            temp.value = text;
            document.body.appendChild(temp);
            temp.select();
            document.execCommand("copy");
            temp.remove();
        }
        const original = copyLogsBtn.textContent;
        copyLogsBtn.textContent = "Copied!";
        setTimeout(() => { copyLogsBtn.textContent = original; }, 1500);
    } catch (err) {
        addLog("ERROR", "LOGS", "Failed to copy logs.", err.message);
    }
}

// ---------------------------
// Multi-Session Management (SQLite)
// ---------------------------
function generateClientSessionId() {
    if (window.crypto && typeof window.crypto.randomUUID === "function") {
        return window.crypto.randomUUID().replace(/-/g, "");
    }
    return `sess_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`;
}

function loadOrCreateSessionId() {
    try {
        const existing = localStorage.getItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY);
        if (existing && existing.trim()) return existing.trim();
        const created = generateClientSessionId();
        localStorage.setItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY, created);
        return created;
    } catch (err) {
        return generateClientSessionId();
    }
}

async function fetchAndRenderSessions() {
    try {
        const resp = await fetch("/chat/sessions");
        if (!resp.ok) return;
        const data = await resp.json();
        const sessions = data.sessions || [];

        sessionList.innerHTML = "";
        if (sessions.length === 0) {
            const empty = document.createElement("div");
            empty.className = "session-item";
            empty.textContent = "No saved chats yet.";
            sessionList.appendChild(empty);
            return;
        }

        sessions.forEach((s) => {
            const item = document.createElement("div");
            item.className = `session-item ${s.id === currentSessionId ? "active" : ""}`;
            item.dataset.sessionId = s.id;

            const titleSpan = document.createElement("span");
            titleSpan.className = "session-item-title";
            titleSpan.textContent = s.title || "New Chat";
            titleSpan.title = s.title;

            const delBtn = document.createElement("button");
            delBtn.className = "session-delete-btn";
            delBtn.innerHTML = "&times;";
            delBtn.title = "Delete chat";
            delBtn.addEventListener("click", async (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                await deleteSession(s.id);
            });

            item.appendChild(titleSpan);
            item.appendChild(delBtn);

            item.addEventListener("click", () => {
                switchSession(s.id);
            });

            sessionList.appendChild(item);
        });
    } catch (err) {
        addLog("WARN", "SESSION", "Could not load sessions list.", err.message);
    }
}

async function switchSession(sessionId) {
    if (isSending || isUploading) return;
    currentSessionId = sessionId;
    localStorage.setItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY, sessionId);

    clearPendingAttachment();
    closeSettingsPage();
    if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
        chatSidebar.classList.add("collapsed");
    }

    await restoreSessionHistory();
    fetchAndRenderSessions();
}

async function deleteSession(sessionId) {
    try {
        await fetch(`/chat/sessions/${encodeURIComponent(sessionId)}`, { method: "DELETE" });
        addLog("INFO", "SESSION", `Deleted session: ${sessionId}`);
        if (currentSessionId === sessionId) {
            await startNewChat();
        } else {
            await fetchAndRenderSessions();
        }
    } catch (err) {
        addLog("ERROR", "SESSION", "Failed to delete session.", err.message);
    }
}

async function startNewChat() {
    if (isSending || isUploading) return;
    const previousSessionId = currentSessionId;
    currentSessionId = generateClientSessionId();
    localStorage.setItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY, currentSessionId);

    clearPendingAttachment();
    chatHistory.innerHTML = "";
    chatHistory.appendChild(emptyStateTemplate.cloneNode(true));
    closeSettingsPage();
    messageInput.focus();

    if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
        chatSidebar.classList.add("collapsed");
    }

    addLog("INFO", "SESSION", "Started new chat session.", {
        previousSessionId,
        newSessionId: currentSessionId,
    });
    fetchAndRenderSessions();
}

async function restoreSessionHistory() {
    try {
        const response = await fetch(`/chat/history/${encodeURIComponent(currentSessionId)}`);
        if (!response.ok) return;
        const data = await response.json();
        const messages = data.messages || [];

        chatHistory.innerHTML = "";
        if (messages.length === 0) {
            chatHistory.appendChild(emptyStateTemplate.cloneNode(true));
        } else {
            for (const msg of messages) {
                if (msg.role === "user" || msg.role === "assistant") {
                    appendMessage(msg.role, msg.content || "");
                }
            }
        }
    } catch (err) {
        addLog("WARN", "SESSION", "Could not restore session history.", err.message);
    }
}

// ---------------------------
// Settings
// ---------------------------
function loadSettings() {
    try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
        if (!raw) return { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS };
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (typeof parsed.ollamaBaseUrl === "string" && parsed.ollamaBaseUrl.includes(".cloudshell.dev")) {
            parsed.ollamaBaseUrl = "";
        }
        return { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, ...parsed };
    } catch (err) {
        return { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS };
    }
}

function saveSettingsToStorage(settings) {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(settings));
}

function getActiveModelName(settings) {
    if (settings.provider === "ollama") return settings.ollamaModel || "not selected";
    if (settings.openrouterModel === "openrouter/free" && settings.pinnedOpenRouterModel) {
        return `${settings.pinnedOpenRouterModel} (pinned)`;
    }
    return settings.openrouterModel || DEFAULT_SETTINGS.openrouterModel;
}

function updateHeaderBadge() {
    const providerLabel = currentSettings.provider === "ollama" ? "Ollama" : "OpenRouter";
    const modelName = getActiveModelName(currentSettings);
    providerBadge.textContent = `${providerLabel} · ${modelName}`;
}

function showStatusBanner(element, message, type) {
    element.textContent = message;
    element.className = `status-banner ${type}`;
    element.classList.remove("hidden");
}

function toggleProviderSections(provider) {
    const isOllama = provider === "ollama";
    openrouterSection.classList.toggle("hidden", isOllama);
    ollamaSection.classList.toggle("hidden", !isOllama);
}

function populateSettingsForm(settings) {
    providerRadios.forEach((radio) => {
        radio.checked = radio.value === settings.provider;
    });
    toggleProviderSections(settings.provider);

    openrouterApiKeyInput.value = settings.openrouterApiKey;
    openrouterModelInput.value = settings.openrouterModel;
    pinnedFreeModelInput.value = settings.pinnedOpenRouterModel || "";
    pinnedVisionModelInput.value = settings.pinnedVisionModel || "";
    ollamaBaseUrlInput.value = settings.ollamaBaseUrl || "";
    ollamaCustomModelInput.value = settings.ollamaModel;

    if (settings.ollamaModel) {
        const exists = Array.from(ollamaModelSelect.options).some(
            (opt) => opt.value === settings.ollamaModel
        );
        if (!exists) {
            const option = document.createElement("option");
            option.value = settings.ollamaModel;
            option.textContent = `${settings.ollamaModel} (saved)`;
            ollamaModelSelect.appendChild(option);
        }
        ollamaModelSelect.value = settings.ollamaModel;
    } else {
        ollamaModelSelect.value = "";
    }
}

function openSettingsPage() {
    populateSettingsForm(currentSettings);
    settingsSaveFeedback.classList.add("hidden");
    chatView.classList.add("hidden");
    settingsView.classList.remove("hidden");
}

function closeSettingsPage() {
    settingsView.classList.add("hidden");
    chatView.classList.remove("hidden");
    messageInput.focus();
}

function formatBytes(bytes) {
    if (!bytes || Number.isNaN(bytes)) return "";
    const gb = bytes / (1024 ** 3);
    if (gb >= 1) return `${gb.toFixed(1)} GB`;
    const mb = bytes / (1024 ** 2);
    if (mb >= 1) return `${mb.toFixed(1)} MB`;
    return `${Math.max(1, Math.round(bytes / 1024))} KB`;
}

async function discoverOllamaModels() {
    let baseUrl = ollamaBaseUrlInput.value.trim();
    discoverOllamaBtn.disabled = true;
    discoverOllamaBtn.textContent = "Scanning...";
    showStatusBanner(ollamaDiscoveryStatus, "Contacting Ollama...", "info");

    try {
        const endpoint = baseUrl
            ? `/api/ollama/models?base_url=${encodeURIComponent(baseUrl)}`
            : "/api/ollama/models";
        const response = await fetch(endpoint);
        const data = await response.json();

        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || `HTTP ${response.status}`);

        ollamaModelSelect.innerHTML = "";
        if (!data.models || data.models.length === 0) {
            const emptyOpt = document.createElement("option");
            emptyOpt.value = "";
            emptyOpt.textContent = "-- No local models found in Ollama --";
            ollamaModelSelect.appendChild(emptyOpt);
            showStatusBanner(ollamaDiscoveryStatus, "Connected, 0 models found.", "info");
            return;
        }

        for (const model of data.models) {
            const opt = document.createElement("option");
            opt.value = model.name;
            const sizeLabel = formatBytes(model.size);
            opt.textContent = sizeLabel ? `${model.name} (${sizeLabel})` : model.name;
            ollamaModelSelect.appendChild(opt);
        }

        const preferred = ollamaCustomModelInput.value.trim() || data.models[0].name;
        const hasPreferred = data.models.some((m) => m.name === preferred);
        ollamaModelSelect.value = hasPreferred ? preferred : data.models[0].name;
        ollamaCustomModelInput.value = ollamaModelSelect.value;
        showStatusBanner(ollamaDiscoveryStatus, `Discovered ${data.count} model(s).`, "success");
    } catch (err) {
        showStatusBanner(ollamaDiscoveryStatus, `Discovery failed: ${err.message}`, "error");
    } finally {
        discoverOllamaBtn.disabled = false;
        discoverOllamaBtn.textContent = "Discover Models";
    }
}

async function clearPinnedModel() {
    currentSettings.pinnedOpenRouterModel = "";
    currentSettings.pinnedVisionModel = "";
    saveSettingsToStorage(currentSettings);
    pinnedFreeModelInput.value = "";
    pinnedVisionModelInput.value = "";
    updateHeaderBadge();
    try {
        await fetch("/chat/reset-model", { method: "POST" });
        showStatusBanner(settingsSaveFeedback, "Pinned models cleared.", "info");
    } catch (err) {}
}

// ---------------------------
// Attachments
// ---------------------------
function clearPendingAttachment() {
    pendingAttachment = null;
    attachmentFileInput.value = "";
    attachmentThumb.src = "";
    attachmentThumb.classList.add("hidden");
    attachmentDocBadge.classList.add("hidden");
    attachmentName.textContent = "";
    attachmentDetails.textContent = "";
    attachmentPreview.classList.add("hidden");
    updateSendButtonState();
}

function showPendingImage(imageInfo) {
    pendingAttachment = { kind: "image", data: imageInfo };
    attachmentDocBadge.classList.add("hidden");
    attachmentThumb.src = imageInfo.url;
    attachmentThumb.classList.remove("hidden");
    attachmentName.textContent = imageInfo.original_filename;
    attachmentDetails.textContent = `${imageInfo.width}x${imageInfo.height} · ${formatBytes(imageInfo.size_bytes)}`;
    attachmentPreview.classList.remove("hidden");
    updateSendButtonState();
}

function showPendingDocument(docInfo) {
    pendingAttachment = { kind: "document", data: docInfo };
    attachmentThumb.classList.add("hidden");
    attachmentDocBadge.textContent = (docInfo.file_type || "DOC").toUpperCase();
    attachmentDocBadge.classList.remove("hidden");
    attachmentName.textContent = docInfo.original_filename;
    const trunc = docInfo.truncated ? " · truncated" : "";
    attachmentDetails.textContent = `${docInfo.page_count} page(s) · ${docInfo.char_count.toLocaleString()} chars${trunc}`;
    attachmentPreview.classList.remove("hidden");
    updateSendButtonState();
}

async function handleAttachmentSelected(file) {
    if (!file || isUploading || isSending) return;
    const ext = "." + file.name.split(".").pop().toLowerCase();
    const isDoc = DOC_EXTENSIONS.includes(ext);
    const endpoint = isDoc ? "/upload/document" : "/upload/image";

    isUploading = true;
    attachFileBtn.disabled = true;
    updateSendButtonState();

    try {
        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append("file", file);
        const response = await fetch(endpoint, { method: "POST", body: formData });
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || `HTTP ${response.status}`);
        if (isDoc) showPendingDocument(data);
        else showPendingImage(data);
    } catch (err) {
        clearPendingAttachment();
        appendMessage("assistant", `Upload failed: ${err.message}`, "error");
    } finally {
        isUploading = false;
        attachFileBtn.disabled = false;
        updateSendButtonState();
    }
}

// ---------------------------
// Chat UI
// ---------------------------
function updateSendButtonState() {
    const hasText = messageInput.value.trim().length > 0;
    const hasAttachment = Boolean(pendingAttachment);
    sendButton.disabled = isSending || isUploading || (!hasText && !hasAttachment);
}

function autoResizeInput() {
    messageInput.style.height = "auto";
    messageInput.style.height = `${Math.min(messageInput.scrollHeight, 160)}px`;
}

function scrollToBottom() {
    chatHistory.scrollTop = chatHistory.scrollHeight;
}

function renderOutputIntoBubble(bubble, output) {
    bubble.innerHTML = "";
    if (output && output.text) {
        const textNode = document.createElement("div");
        textNode.textContent = output.text;
        bubble.appendChild(textNode);
    }
    if (output && output.used_a2ui && window.OracleA2uiRenderer) {
        const surfaceRoot = window.OracleA2uiRenderer.renderA2uiMessages(
            output.a2ui_messages,
            handleA2uiAction
        );
        bubble.appendChild(surfaceRoot);
    }
}

function appendMessage(role, text, extraClass = "", attachment = null, output = null) {
    const emptyEl = document.getElementById("empty-state");
    if (emptyEl) emptyEl.remove();

    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = `message-row ${role}`;

    const roleLabel = document.createElement("span");
    roleLabel.className = "message-role";
    roleLabel.textContent = role === "user" ? "You" : "Assistant";

    const bubble = document.createElement("div");
    bubble.className = extraClass ? `message-bubble ${extraClass}` : "message-bubble";

    if (attachment && attachment.kind === "image" && attachment.data.url) {
        const img = document.createElement("img");
        img.className = "chat-image-preview";
        img.src = attachment.data.url;
        bubble.appendChild(img);
    } else if (attachment && attachment.kind === "document") {
        const pill = document.createElement("div");
        pill.className = "chat-doc-pill";
        pill.textContent = `[${(attachment.data.file_type || "DOC").toUpperCase()}] ${attachment.data.original_filename}`;
        bubble.appendChild(pill);
    }

    if (output) renderOutputIntoBubble(bubble, output);
    else if (text) {
        const t = document.createElement("div");
        t.textContent = text;
        bubble.appendChild(t);
    }

    row.appendChild(roleLabel);
    row.appendChild(bubble);
    chatHistory.appendChild(row);
    scrollToBottom();
    return bubble;
}

async function handleA2uiAction(actionPayload) {
    if (isSending || isUploading) return;
    const actionMessage = `UI Action "${actionPayload.name}" submitted with values: ${JSON.stringify(actionPayload.context)}`;
    await sendChatRequest({
        promptText: actionMessage,
        a2uiAction: actionPayload,
        userDisplayText: `[A2UI Action: ${actionPayload.name}] ${JSON.stringify(actionPayload.context)}`,
    });
}

async function runA2uiShowcaseDemo() {
    closeSettingsPage();
    try {
        const response = await fetch("/api/a2ui/showcase");
        const data = await response.json();
        appendMessage("assistant", "", "", null, data.output);
    } catch (err) {
        appendMessage("assistant", `Showcase error: ${err.message}`, "error");
    }
}

async function sendChatRequest({
    promptText,
    attached = null,
    a2uiAction = null,
    userDisplayText = null,
}) {
    const isVision = Boolean(attached && attached.kind === "image");
    const isDocument = Boolean(attached && attached.kind === "document");
    const uiMode = uiModeSelect.value || "auto";
    const activeProvider = currentSettings.provider || "openrouter";

    appendMessage("user", userDisplayText || promptText, "", attached);
    isSending = true;
    updateSendButtonState();

    const routerModel = activeProvider === "ollama"
        ? currentSettings.ollamaModel
        : currentSettings.openrouterModel || DEFAULT_SETTINGS.openrouterModel;

    const activePin = activeProvider === "openrouter" && routerModel === "openrouter/free"
        ? isVision ? currentSettings.pinnedVisionModel : currentSettings.pinnedOpenRouterModel
        : "";

    const effectiveDisplay = activePin || routerModel || activeProvider;
    const pendingBubble = appendMessage("assistant", `Thinking (${effectiveDisplay})...`, "pending");

    const requestBody = {
        message: promptText,
        session_id: currentSessionId,
        provider: activeProvider,
        model: routerModel,
        ui_mode: uiMode,
    };
    if (isVision) requestBody.image_id = attached.data.file_id;
    if (isDocument) {
        requestBody.document_id = attached.data.file_id;
        requestBody.document_name = attached.data.original_filename;
    }
    if (a2uiAction) requestBody.a2ui_action = a2uiAction;
    if (activeProvider === "openrouter") {
        if (currentSettings.openrouterApiKey) requestBody.api_key = currentSettings.openrouterApiKey;
    } else if (activeProvider === "ollama" && currentSettings.ollamaBaseUrl) {
        requestBody.ollama_base_url = currentSettings.ollamaBaseUrl;
    }

    try {
        const response = await fetch("/chat", {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify(requestBody),
        });

        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || `HTTP ${response.status}`);

        if (data.session_id) {
            currentSessionId = data.session_id;
            localStorage.setItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY, currentSessionId);
        }

        if (activeProvider === "openrouter" && routerModel === "openrouter/free" && data.model) {
            if (data.vision_used && currentSettings.pinnedVisionModel !== data.model) {
                currentSettings.pinnedVisionModel = data.model;
                saveSettingsToStorage(currentSettings);
                pinnedVisionModelInput.value = data.model;
            } else if (!data.vision_used && currentSettings.pinnedOpenRouterModel !== data.model) {
                currentSettings.pinnedOpenRouterModel = data.model;
                saveSettingsToStorage(currentSettings);
                pinnedFreeModelInput.value = data.model;
                updateHeaderBadge();
            }
        }

        pendingBubble.classList.remove("pending");
        renderOutputIntoBubble(pendingBubble, data.output);
        fetchAndRenderSessions();
    } catch (err) {
        pendingBubble.classList.remove("pending");
        pendingBubble.classList.add("error");
        pendingBubble.textContent = `Error: ${err.message}`;
    } finally {
        isSending = false;
        updateSendButtonState();
        messageInput.focus();
        scrollToBottom();
    }
}

async function handleSend(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const rawText = messageInput.value.trim();
    const attached = pendingAttachment;
    if ((!rawText && !attached) || isSending || isUploading) return;

    let promptText = rawText || (attached && attached.kind === "image"
        ? "Describe this image in detail."
        : "Summarize this document and list its key points.");

    messageInput.value = "";
    autoResizeInput();
    if (attached) clearPendingAttachment();

    await sendChatRequest({ promptText, attached });
}

// ---------------------------
// Event Listeners
// ---------------------------
messageInput.addEventListener("input", () => {
    autoResizeInput();
    updateSendButtonState();
});

messageInput.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
    if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey) {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!sendButton.disabled) chatForm.requestSubmit();
    }
});

chatForm.addEventListener("submit", handleSend);

sidebarToggleBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
    chatSidebar.classList.toggle("collapsed");
});

sidebarCloseBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
    chatSidebar.classList.add("collapsed");
});

sidebarNewChatBtn.addEventListener("click", startNewChat);
newChatBtn.addEventListener("click", startNewChat);
a2uiDemoBtn.addEventListener("click", runA2uiShowcaseDemo);
settingsBtn.addEventListener("click", openSettingsPage);
backToChatBtn.addEventListener("click", closeSettingsPage);

attachFileBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
    if (!isUploading && !isSending) attachmentFileInput.click();
});

attachmentFileInput.addEventListener("change", () => {
    const file = attachmentFileInput.files && attachmentFileInput.files[0];
    if (file) handleAttachmentSelected(file);
});

removeAttachmentBtn.addEventListener("click", clearPendingAttachment);

providerRadios.forEach((r) => {
    r.addEventListener("change", (e) => toggleProviderSections(e.target.value));
});

toggleKeyBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
    const isPwd = openrouterApiKeyInput.type === "password";
    openrouterApiKeyInput.type = isPwd ? "text" : "password";
    toggleKeyBtn.textContent = isPwd ? "Hide" : "Show";
});

discoverOllamaBtn.addEventListener("click", discoverOllamaModels);
clearPinnedBtn.addEventListener("click", clearPinnedModel);

settingsForm.addEventListener("submit", (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const provider = document.querySelector('input[name="provider"]:checked').value;
    currentSettings = {
        provider,
        openrouterApiKey: openrouterApiKeyInput.value.trim(),
        openrouterModel: openrouterModelInput.value.trim() || DEFAULT_SETTINGS.openrouterModel,
        pinnedOpenRouterModel: currentSettings.pinnedOpenRouterModel,
        pinnedVisionModel: currentSettings.pinnedVisionModel,
        ollamaBaseUrl: ollamaBaseUrlInput.value.trim(),
        ollamaModel: ollamaCustomModelInput.value.trim() || ollamaModelSelect.value,
    };
    saveSettingsToStorage(currentSettings);
    updateHeaderBadge();
    showStatusBanner(settingsSaveFeedback, "Settings saved.", "success");
});

resetSettingsBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
    currentSettings = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS };
    saveSettingsToStorage(currentSettings);
    populateSettingsForm(currentSettings);
    updateHeaderBadge();
    showStatusBanner(settingsSaveFeedback, "Reset to defaults.", "info");
});

logsToggleBtn.addEventListener("click", () => toggleLogDrawer());
openLogsFromSettingsBtn.addEventListener("click", () => toggleLogDrawer(true));
closeLogsBtn.addEventListener("click", () => toggleLogDrawer(false));
copyLogsBtn.addEventListener("click", copyLogsToClipboard);
clearLogsBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
    logEntries.length = 0;
    renderLogs();
});

logFilterButtons.forEach((btn) => {
    btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        activeLogFilter = btn.dataset.filter;
        logFilterButtons.forEach((b) => b.classList.toggle("active", b === btn));
        renderLogs();
    });
});

// Initialization
updateHeaderBadge();
restoreSessionHistory();
fetchAndRenderSessions();

=== END FILE ===

=== FILE: .env.example ===
# Application Settings
APP_HOST=0.0.0.0
APP_PORT=8080

# LLM Configuration
LLM_PROVIDER=openrouter
MODEL_NAME=openrouter/free
OPENROUTER_API_KEY=your_openrouter_api_key_here
OPENROUTER_BASE_URL=https://openrouter.ai/api/v1
OLLAMA_BASE_URL=http://localhost:11434
OLLAMA_TIMEOUT=300

=== END FILE ===

=== FILE: .github/workflows/deploy.yml ===
name: Auto Deploy to Oracle VM

on:
  push:
    branches:
      - main

jobs:
  deploy:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - name: Deploy to Oracle VM
        uses: appleboy/ssh-action@v1.2.0
        with:
          host: ${{ secrets.VM_HOST }}
          username: ${{ secrets.VM_USER }}
          key: ${{ secrets.VM_SSH_KEY }}
          script: |
            cd /home/ubuntu/oracle_chat
            git fetch origin main
            git reset --hard origin/main
            
            source venv/bin/activate
            pip install -r requirements.txt
            
            # Copy and enable the new service
            sudo cp deploy/oracle-bot.service /etc/systemd/system/oracle-bot.service
            sudo systemctl daemon-reload
            sudo systemctl enable oracle-bot
            sudo systemctl restart oracle-bot
            
            # Restart your main web app too
            sudo systemctl restart oracle-chat

=== END FILE ===

=== FILE: PLAN.md ===
# ORACLE_CHAT PROJECT PLAN#

Repository:
https://github.com/drshariqalikhan/oracle_chat

Author:
Dr Shariq Ali Khan

Primary Development Environment:
Google Cloud Shell

Source Control:
GitHub (single source of truth)

Target Deployment:
Oracle Cloud Always Free VM

Long-Term Goal:
A self-hosted AI chat application with support for:

- Chat
- Image upload
- Vision-capable LLMs
- PDF and document upload
- Multiple interchangeable LLM providers
- Local Ollama models
- Agent extensions in future releases

---

# 1. PROJECT VISION

ORACLE_CHAT is intended to be a lightweight, self-hosted AI assistant platform that is developed entirely in Google Cloud Shell and deployed later to an Oracle Always Free VM.

The system should support:

- Text chat
- Image understanding
- Document understanding
- Multiple LLM providers
- Local LLM inference through Ollama
- Future agentic workflows

The architecture must prioritize simplicity, maintainability, and swap-able AI providers.

---

# 2. DEVELOPMENT CONSTRAINTS

The coding AI must always assume:

1. Developer is a hobby coder.
2. Development occurs entirely inside Google Cloud Shell.
3. No local development PC workflow.
4. GitHub is the source of truth.
5. All code changes should be provided as executable Bash commands.
6. Files should be created using:

   cat << 'EOF' > filename

7. Never provide partial file contents.
8. Always provide complete file contents.
9. Every milestone should end with a working application state.
10. Commit and push after every milestone.

---

# 3. HIGH LEVEL ARCHITECTURE

Browser
    ↓
Web Chat Interface
    ↓
FastAPI Backend
    ↓
Provider Abstraction Layer
        ↓
        OpenRouter
        Ollama
        Future Providers
    ↓
Selected Model

---

# 4. TECHNOLOGY STACK

Frontend

- HTML
- CSS
- Vanilla JavaScript

Backend

- Python
- FastAPI
- Uvicorn

AI Providers

- OpenRouter
- Ollama

File Processing

- Pillow
- PyPDF

Version Control

- Git
- GitHub

Future Components

- SQLite
- Vector Database
- Agent Framework
- Authentication Layer

---

# 5. PROJECT DIRECTORY STRUCTURE

oracle_chat/

├── app/

├── backend/
│   ├── main.py

│   │   └── ollama.py
│   │

│   │   ├── chat.py
│   │   ├── upload.py
│   │   └── health.py
│   │

│       ├── file_service.py
│       └── image_service.py
│
├── frontend/
│   ├── templates/
│   │   └── index.html
│   │

│       ├── css/

---

# 6. DEVELOPMENT PHILOSOPHY

The project must be developed using incremental milestones.

Each milestone should:

- Produce a working application
- Be independently testable
- Be committed to GitHub
- Preserve compatibility with future milestones

No milestone should require major rewrites of earlier milestones.

---

# 7. MILESTONE ROADMAP

## MILESTONE 0
Repository Bootstrap

Goal:
Create base project structure and verify FastAPI works.

Deliverables:
- Folder structure
- requirements.txt
- main.py
- Home route
- Uvicorn launch

Success Criteria:
Browser displays:

ORACLE CHAT
System Online

Git Commit:

milestone-0-bootstrap

---

## MILESTONE 1
Modern Chat Interface

Goal:
Create ChatGPT-style frontend.

Features:

- Header
- Chat history
- User messages
- Assistant messages
- Input field
- Send button

Backend:
No AI connection.

Success Criteria:
Messages display in browser.

Git Commit:

milestone-1-chat-ui

---

## MILESTONE 2
Frontend ↔ Backend Integration

Goal:
Connect UI to FastAPI.

Endpoint:

POST /chat

Request:

{
  "message": "hello"
}

Response:

{
  "reply": "message received"
}

Success Criteria:
Backend receives and returns messages.

Git Commit:

milestone-2-chat-api

---

## MILESTONE 3
OpenRouter Integration

Goal:
Connect first LLM.

Files:

backend/llm/base.py

backend/llm/openrouter.py

Environment Variable:

OPENROUTER_API_KEY

Success Criteria:
Messages routed through OpenRouter.

Git Commit:

milestone-3-openrouter

---

## MILESTONE 4
Conversation Memory

Goal:
Maintain conversation history.

Features:

- Session-based memory
- Message history

Success Criteria:
Model remembers earlier messages.

Git Commit:

milestone-4-memory

---

## MILESTONE 5
Image Upload

Goal:
Allow image uploads.

Supported Types:

- jpg
- jpeg
- png
- webp

Features:

- Upload image
- Store image
- Display preview

Success Criteria:
Image visible inside chat flow.

Git Commit:

milestone-5-image-upload

---

## MILESTONE 6
Vision Support

Goal:
Analyze images using multimodal LLM.

Workflow:

Upload image
→ Vision model
→ Analysis returned

Success Criteria:
Image descriptions generated.

Git Commit:

milestone-6-vision

---

## MILESTONE 7
Document Upload

Goal:
Support:

- PDF
- TXT

Workflow:

Upload
→ Extract Text
→ Send To Model

Success Criteria:
Questions can be answered about documents.

Git Commit:

milestone-7-documents

---

## MILESTONE 8
Provider Abstraction Layer

Goal:
Support multiple inference providers.

Create Interface:

LLMProvider

Methods:

- chat()
- analyze_image()
- process_document()

Implementations:

- OpenRouterProvider
- OllamaProvider

Success Criteria:

Backend can switch providers through configuration.

Git Commit:

milestone-8-provider-abstraction

---

## MILESTONE 9
Ollama Integration

Goal:
Connect local models.

Example Models:

- gemma3:4b
- qwen2.5-vl

Success Criteria:
Application works against local Ollama API.

Git Commit:

milestone-9-ollama

---

## MILESTONE 10
Oracle VM Deployment

Goal:
Deploy production environment.

Tasks:

- Install Python
- Install Ollama
- Configure Firewall
- Configure Environment Variables
- Configure Startup Service

Success Criteria:
Public deployment operational.

Git Commit:

milestone-10-oracle-deployment

---

## MILESTONE 11
Runtime Model Switching

Goal:
Switch providers and models without code changes.

Examples:

LLM_PROVIDER=openrouter

MODEL_NAME=google/gemma-4-31b-it:free

OR

LLM_PROVIDER=ollama

MODEL_NAME=qwen2.5-vl

Success Criteria:
Only environment variables change.

Git Commit:

milestone-11-model-switching

---

# 8. TESTING STRATEGY

Each milestone must include:

- Startup verification
- Browser verification
- API verification
- Error handling verification

The application must never regress.

---

# ORACLE_CHAT

Self-hosted AI chat application designed for development in Google Cloud Shell and deployment on Oracle Cloud Always Free VM.

See `PLAN.md` for the full architecture and milestone roadmap.

## Quick Start (Milestone 0)

    python3 -m venv venv
    source venv/bin/activate
    pip install -r requirements.txt
    uvicorn backend.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8080 --reload


he cleanest and fastest way is to generate a single bundled text file of all your current code files right from Google Cloud Shell.
Run this one-liner in your Google Cloud Shell terminal:
Bash
cd ~/oracle_chat
git pull origin main

python3 -c "
import subprocess

files = subprocess.check_output(['git', 'ls-files']).decode().splitlines()
skip_ext = {'.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.webp', '.pdf', '.keep'}

with open('project_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as out:
    for f in sorted(files):
        if any(f.endswith(ext) for ext in skip_ext):
            continue
        out.write(f'=== FILE: {f} ===\n')
        try:
            with open(f, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as fh:
                out.write(fh.read())
        except Exception as e:
            out.write(f'ERROR: {e}\n')
        out.write('\n=== END FILE ===\n\n')

print('Export complete! Check project_dump.txt')
"


Switch to OpenRouter on the fly:
Text
/provider openrouter
What happens: The bot confirms the switch. Your next messages instantly route to OpenRouter (openrouter/free) in the cloud.
Switch back to Ollama and specify your exact model:
Text
/provider ollama
/model gemma4:e4b-it-qat
What happens: The bot locks onto your local Gemma 4 model with zero service restarts required.

=== FILE: backend/config.py ===
import os
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from dotenv import load_dotenv

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
load_dotenv(BASE_DIR / ".env")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Settings:
    app_host: str
    app_port: int
    llm_provider: str
    model_name: str
    openrouter_api_key: str
    openrouter_base_url: str
    ollama_base_url: str
    ollama_timeout: float


def get_settings() -> Settings:
    return Settings(
        app_host=os.getenv("APP_HOST", "0.0.0.0"),
        app_port=int(os.getenv("APP_PORT", "8080")),
        llm_provider=os.getenv("LLM_PROVIDER", "openrouter").strip().lower(),
        model_name=os.getenv("MODEL_NAME", "openrouter/free").strip(),
        openrouter_api_key=os.getenv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY", "").strip(),
        openrouter_base_url=os.getenv(
            "OPENROUTER_BASE_URL", "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1"
        ).strip().rstrip("/"),
        ollama_base_url=os.getenv("OLLAMA_BASE_URL", "http://localhost:11434").strip().rstrip("/"),
        ollama_timeout=float(os.getenv("OLLAMA_TIMEOUT", "300")),
    )
