=== FILE: backend/llm/base.py ===
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any


class LLMProviderError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, message: str, status_code: int = 502) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.message = message
        self.status_code = status_code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LLMResponse:
    content: str
    model: str


class LLMProvider(ABC):
    @abstractmethod
    async def chat(
        self,
        messages: list[dict[str, Any]],
        model: str | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        """Send chat messages and return an LLMResponse containing text and resolved model."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def analyze_image(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        image_data_url: str,
        history: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
        model: str | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        """Analyze an image using a multimodal model and return an LLMResponse."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def process_document(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        document_context: str,
        history: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
        model: str | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        """Answer a prompt grounded in extracted document text."""
        raise NotImplementedError

=== END FILE ===

=== FILE: backend/llm/factory.py ===
from backend.config import Settings
from backend.llm.base import LLMProvider, LLMProviderError
from backend.llm.ollama import OllamaProvider
from backend.llm.openrouter import OpenRouterProvider

SUPPORTED_PROVIDERS = ("openrouter", "ollama")


def create_llm_provider(
    settings: Settings,
    provider_name: str | None = None,
    api_key: str | None = None,
    default_model: str | None = None,
    ollama_base_url: str | None = None,
) -> tuple[str, LLMProvider]:
    selected_provider = (provider_name or settings.llm_provider or "openrouter").strip().lower()

    if selected_provider == "openrouter":
        resolved_key = (api_key or "").strip() or settings.openrouter_api_key
        resolved_model = (default_model or "").strip() or settings.model_name or "openrouter/free"
        return selected_provider, OpenRouterProvider(
            api_key=resolved_key,
            default_model=resolved_model,
            base_url=settings.openrouter_base_url,
        )

    if selected_provider == "ollama":
        resolved_url = (ollama_base_url or "").strip().rstrip("/") or settings.ollama_base_url
        env_default = "" if settings.model_name == "openrouter/free" else settings.model_name
        resolved_model = (default_model or "").strip() or env_default
        return selected_provider, OllamaProvider(
            base_url=resolved_url,
            default_model=resolved_model,
            timeout=settings.ollama_timeout,
        )

    raise LLMProviderError(
        f"Unsupported LLM provider '{selected_provider}'. Supported providers: {', '.join(SUPPORTED_PROVIDERS)}.",
        status_code=400,
    )

=== END FILE ===

=== FILE: backend/llm/ollama.py ===
import re
from typing import Any
from urllib.parse import urlparse

import httpx

from backend.llm.base import LLMProvider, LLMProviderError, LLMResponse

CLOUDSHELL_PREVIEW_RE = re.compile(
    r"^https?://(\d+)-cs-[^/]+\.cloudshell\.dev(?:/.*)?$",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)


def normalize_ollama_base_url(base_url: str) -> str:
    cleaned = (base_url or "").strip().rstrip("/")
    match = CLOUDSHELL_PREVIEW_RE.match(cleaned)
    if match:
        port = match.group(1)
        return f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"
    return cleaned


class OllamaProvider(LLMProvider):
    def __init__(
        self,
        base_url: str = "http://localhost:11434",
        default_model: str = "",
        timeout: float = 300.0,
    ) -> None:
        self.base_url = normalize_ollama_base_url(base_url or "http://localhost:11434")
        self.default_model = default_model.strip()
        self.timeout = timeout

    def _validate_base_url(self) -> None:
        parsed = urlparse(self.base_url)
        if parsed.scheme not in ("http", "https") or not parsed.netloc:
            raise LLMProviderError(
                f"Invalid Ollama URL: '{self.base_url}'. Must start with http:// or https://",
                status_code=400,
            )

    async def list_models(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        self._validate_base_url()
        tags_url = f"{self.base_url}/api/tags"

        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=8.0) as client:
                response = await client.get(tags_url, headers={"Accept": "application/json"})
        except httpx.TimeoutException as exc:
            raise LLMProviderError(
                f"Timed out connecting to Ollama at {self.base_url}",
                status_code=504,
            ) from exc
        except httpx.RequestError as exc:
            raise LLMProviderError(
                f"Cannot reach Ollama at {self.base_url} ({exc})",
                status_code=502,
            ) from exc

        if response.status_code >= 400:
            detail = self._extract_error_detail(response)
            raise LLMProviderError(
                f"Ollama returned HTTP {response.status_code} at {tags_url}: {detail}",
                status_code=502,
            )

        try:
            payload = response.json()
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise LLMProviderError(
                f"Invalid JSON received from Ollama at {tags_url}.",
                status_code=502,
            ) from exc

        raw_models = payload.get("models", [])
        return [
            {
                "name": item.get("name", ""),
                "size": item.get("size", 0),
                "modified_at": item.get("modified_at", ""),
                "details": item.get("details", {}),
            }
            for item in raw_models
            if item.get("name")
        ]

    async def _auto_detect_model(self) -> str:
        models = await self.list_models()
        if not models:
            raise LLMProviderError(
                "No local Ollama models installed. Run `sudo ollama pull <model_name>` on your VM.",
                status_code=400,
            )
        return models[0]["name"]

    async def chat(
        self,
        messages: list[dict[str, Any]],
        model: str | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        self._validate_base_url()
        selected_model = (model or "").strip() or self.default_model
        if not selected_model or selected_model == "openrouter/free":
            selected_model = await self._auto_detect_model()

        url = f"{self.base_url}/api/chat"
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "model": selected_model,
            "messages": messages,
            "stream": False,
            "think": False,  # <--- DISBLES DEEP REASONING / CHAIN OF THOUGHT
            "options": {
                "num_ctx": 4096,
            },
        }

        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=self.timeout) as client:
                response = await client.post(url, json=payload)
        except httpx.TimeoutException as exc:
            raise LLMProviderError(
                f"Timed out waiting for Ollama response at {self.base_url} (model: {selected_model}).",
                status_code=504,
            ) from exc
        except httpx.RequestError as exc:
            raise LLMProviderError(
                f"Cannot reach Ollama at {self.base_url}: {exc}",
                status_code=502,
            ) from exc

        if response.status_code >= 400:
            detail = self._extract_error_detail(response)
            if response.status_code == 404:
                raise LLMProviderError(
                    f"Ollama model '{selected_model}' not found ({detail}). Run: ollama pull {selected_model}",
                    status_code=404,
                )
            mapped_status = 400 if response.status_code == 400 else 502
            raise LLMProviderError(
                f"Ollama error ({response.status_code}): {detail}",
                status_code=mapped_status,
            )

        try:
            data = response.json()
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise LLMProviderError(
                f"Invalid JSON response received from Ollama at {self.base_url}.",
                status_code=502,
            ) from exc

        message_obj = data.get("message") or {}
        content = message_obj.get("content")
        if not isinstance(content, str) or not content.strip():
            raise LLMProviderError(
                f"Ollama returned an empty response for model '{selected_model}'.",
                status_code=502,
            )

        resolved_model = str(data.get("model") or selected_model).strip()
        return LLMResponse(content=content.strip(), model=resolved_model)

    async def analyze_image(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        image_data_url: str,
        history: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
        model: str | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        raw_base64 = (
            image_data_url.split(",", 1)[1]
            if "," in image_data_url
            else image_data_url
        )
        multimodal_message = {
            "role": "user",
            "content": prompt,
            "images": [raw_base64],
        }
        messages = [*(history or []), multimodal_message]
        return await self.chat(messages=messages, model=model, **kwargs)

    async def process_document(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        document_context: str,
        history: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
        model: str | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        context_instruction = {
            "role": "user",
            "content": (
                "Use the following uploaded document content to answer the user's questions accurately. "
                "If the answer is not in the document, say so clearly.\n\n"
                f"{document_context}"
            ),
        }
        messages = [
            context_instruction,
            *(history or []),
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt},
        ]
        return await self.chat(messages=messages, model=model, **kwargs)

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_error_detail(response: httpx.Response) -> str:
        try:
            data = response.json()
            if isinstance(data, dict) and data.get("error"):
                return str(data["error"])
        except ValueError:
            pass
        return response.text.strip() or f"HTTP {response.status_code}"

=== END FILE ===

=== FILE: backend/llm/openrouter.py ===
from typing import Any

import httpx

from backend.llm.base import LLMProvider, LLMProviderError, LLMResponse


class OpenRouterProvider(LLMProvider):
    def __init__(
        self,
        api_key: str,
        default_model: str = "openrouter/free",
        base_url: str = "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1",
        timeout: float = 90.0,
    ) -> None:
        self.api_key = api_key.strip()
        self.default_model = default_model.strip()
        self.base_url = base_url.strip().rstrip("/")
        self.timeout = timeout

    async def chat(
        self,
        messages: list[dict[str, Any]],
        model: str | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        if not self.api_key:
            raise LLMProviderError(
                "OpenRouter API key is missing. Set OPENROUTER_API_KEY in .env or enter it in Settings.",
                status_code=400,
            )

        selected_model = (model or "").strip() or self.default_model
        if not selected_model:
            raise LLMProviderError("No OpenRouter model specified.", status_code=400)

        url = f"{self.base_url}/chat/completions"
        headers = {
            "Authorization": f"Bearer {self.api_key}",
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "HTTP-Referer": "https://github.com/drshariqalikhan/oracle_chat",
            "X-Title": "ORACLE CHAT",
        }
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "model": selected_model,
            "messages": messages,
        }

        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=self.timeout) as client:
                response = await client.post(url, headers=headers, json=payload)
        except httpx.TimeoutException as exc:
            raise LLMProviderError(
                f"Timed out waiting for OpenRouter response (model: {selected_model}).",
                status_code=504,
            ) from exc
        except httpx.RequestError as exc:
            raise LLMProviderError(
                f"Network error contacting OpenRouter: {exc}",
                status_code=502,
            ) from exc

        if response.status_code >= 400:
            detail = self._extract_error_detail(response)
            mapped_status = (
                response.status_code
                if response.status_code in (400, 401, 402, 403, 404, 429)
                else 502
            )
            raise LLMProviderError(
                f"OpenRouter error ({response.status_code}): {detail}",
                status_code=mapped_status,
            )

        try:
            data = response.json()
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise LLMProviderError("Invalid JSON response received from OpenRouter.", status_code=502) from exc

        choices = data.get("choices") or []
        if not choices:
            raise LLMProviderError("OpenRouter returned no choices in response.", status_code=502)

        message_obj = choices[0].get("message") or {}
        content = message_obj.get("content")

        if isinstance(content, list):
            text_parts = [
                part.get("text", "")
                for part in content
                if isinstance(part, dict) and part.get("type") == "text"
            ]
            content = "\n".join(part for part in text_parts if part)

        if not isinstance(content, str) or not content.strip():
            raise LLMProviderError("OpenRouter returned an empty message.", status_code=502)

        assigned_model = str(data.get("model") or selected_model).strip()
        return LLMResponse(content=content.strip(), model=assigned_model)

    async def analyze_image(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        image_data_url: str,
        history: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
        model: str | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        multimodal_message = {
            "role": "user",
            "content": [
                {"type": "text", "text": prompt},
                {"type": "image_url", "image_url": {"url": image_data_url}},
            ],
        }
        messages = [*(history or []), multimodal_message]
        return await self.chat(messages=messages, model=model, **kwargs)

    async def process_document(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        document_context: str,
        history: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
        model: str | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        context_instruction = {
            "role": "user",
            "content": (
                "Use the following uploaded document content to answer the user's questions accurately. "
                "If the answer is not in the document, say so clearly.\n\n"
                f"{document_context}"
            ),
        }
        messages = [
            context_instruction,
            *(history or []),
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt},
        ]
        return await self.chat(messages=messages, model=model, **kwargs)

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_error_detail(response: httpx.Response) -> str:
        try:
            data = response.json()
            if isinstance(data, dict):
                err = data.get("error")
                if isinstance(err, dict) and err.get("message"):
                    return str(err["message"])
                if data.get("message"):
                    return str(data["message"])
        except ValueError:
            pass
        return response.text.strip() or f"HTTP {response.status_code}"

=== END FILE ===

from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles

from backend.config import get_settings
from backend.routes.chat import router as chat_router
from backend.routes.health import router as health_router
from backend.routes.upload import router as upload_router

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
FRONTEND_DIR = BASE_DIR / "frontend"
TEMPLATES_DIR = FRONTEND_DIR / "templates"
STATIC_DIR = FRONTEND_DIR / "static"
UPLOADS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "uploads"
UPLOADS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

get_settings()

app = FastAPI(title="ORACLE CHAT", version="0.9.0")

app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory=str(STATIC_DIR)), name="static")
app.mount("/uploads", StaticFiles(directory=str(UPLOADS_DIR)), name="uploads")
app.include_router(health_router)
app.include_router(chat_router)
app.include_router(upload_router)


@app.get("/")
async def home() -> FileResponse:
    return FileResponse(TEMPLATES_DIR / "index.html")

=== FILE: backend/models.py ===
from typing import Any, Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, model_validator


class A2uiActionPayload(BaseModel):
    name: str
    surface_id: str = "main"
    source_component_id: str | None = None
    context: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)


class StandardizedLLMOutput(BaseModel):
    text: str
    a2ui_messages: list[dict[str, Any]] = Field(default_factory=list)
    used_a2ui: bool = False
    ui_mode: Literal["auto", "a2ui", "text"] = "auto"
    raw_format: Literal["express", "wire_json", "text"] = "text"
    repair_retried: bool = False
    validation_warnings: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)


class ToolTrace(BaseModel):
    step: int
    tool: str
    arguments: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    duration_ms: float
    ok: bool = True
    result_preview: str = ""


class ToolRunRequest(BaseModel):
    arguments: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)


class SessionSummary(BaseModel):
    id: str
    title: str
    created_at: str
    updated_at: str
    message_count: int


class SessionListResponse(BaseModel):
    sessions: list[SessionSummary]


class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
    message: str = ""
    session_id: str | None = None
    image_id: str | None = None
    document_id: str | None = None
    document_name: str | None = None
    ui_mode: Literal["auto", "a2ui", "text"] = "auto"
    enable_tools: bool = True
    a2ui_action: A2uiActionPayload | None = None
    provider: str | None = None
    model: str | None = None
    pinned_model: str | None = None
    pinned_vision_model: str | None = None
    pinned_tool_model: str | None = None
    api_key: str | None = None
    ollama_base_url: str | None = None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_message_or_attachment(self) -> "ChatRequest":
        cleaned_msg = self.message.strip()
        cleaned_img = (self.image_id or "").strip() or None
        cleaned_doc = (self.document_id or "").strip() or None

        if not cleaned_msg and not cleaned_img and not cleaned_doc and not self.a2ui_action:
            raise ValueError("A message, image_id, document_id, or a2ui_action is required.")

        if not cleaned_msg:
            if self.a2ui_action:
                cleaned_msg = (
                    f"User triggered A2UI action '{self.a2ui_action.name}' on surface "
                    f"'{self.a2ui_action.surface_id}' with context: {self.a2ui_action.context}"
                )
            elif cleaned_img:
                cleaned_msg = "Describe this image in detail."
            elif cleaned_doc:
                cleaned_msg = "Summarize this document and list its key points."

        self.message = cleaned_msg
        self.image_id = cleaned_img
        self.document_id = cleaned_doc
        self.document_name = (self.document_name or "").strip() or None
        self.provider = (self.provider or "").strip().lower() or None
        self.ollama_base_url = (self.ollama_base_url or "").strip().rstrip("/") or None
        return self


class ChatResponse(BaseModel):
    reply: str
    output: StandardizedLLMOutput
    session_id: str
    history_length: int
    provider: str
    model: str | None = None
    pinned: bool = False
    vision_used: bool = False
    document_used: bool = False
    tools_used: bool = False
    tool_traces: list[ToolTrace] = Field(default_factory=list)


class ChatHistoryResponse(BaseModel):
    session_id: str
    title: str = "Chat"
    messages: list[dict[str, str]]


class ImageUploadResponse(BaseModel):
    status: str
    file_id: str
    original_filename: str
    url: str
    content_type: str
    size_bytes: int
    width: int
    height: int


class DocumentUploadResponse(BaseModel):
    status: str
    file_id: str
    original_filename: str
    file_type: str
    size_bytes: int
    page_count: int
    char_count: int
    truncated: bool
    preview: str

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Query

from backend.config import get_settings
from backend.llm.base import LLMProvider, LLMProviderError, LLMResponse
from backend.llm.factory import SUPPORTED_PROVIDERS, create_llm_provider
from backend.llm.ollama import OllamaProvider
from backend.models import (
    ChatHistoryResponse,
    ChatRequest,
    ChatResponse,
    SessionListResponse,
    SessionSummary,
    StandardizedLLMOutput,
    ToolRunRequest,
    ToolTrace,
)
from backend.services.a2ui_service import ALL_BASIC_COMPONENTS, A2uiService
from backend.services.agent_service import AgentResult, AgentService
from backend.services.file_service import FileService, FileValidationError
from backend.services.image_service import ImageService, ImageValidationError
from backend.services.memory_service import MemoryService
from backend.tools.registry import create_default_tool_registry

router = APIRouter(tags=["chat"])

FREE_ROUTER_MODEL = "openrouter/free"
RETRIABLE_PIN_STATUSES = {400, 404, 429, 502, 503, 504}
UPLOADS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent / "uploads"

_pinned_free_model: str | None = None
_pinned_vision_model: str | None = None
_pinned_tool_model: str | None = None

memory_service = MemoryService()
image_service = ImageService(uploads_dir=UPLOADS_DIR)
file_service = FileService(uploads_dir=UPLOADS_DIR)
a2ui_service = A2uiService()
tool_registry = create_default_tool_registry(
    memory_service=memory_service,
    file_service=file_service,
    uploads_dir=UPLOADS_DIR,
)
agent_service = AgentService(tool_registry=tool_registry, max_steps=5)


@router.get("/api/providers")
async def get_provider_config() -> dict[str, Any]:
    settings = get_settings()
    return {
        "active_provider": settings.llm_provider,
        "default_model": settings.model_name,
        "ollama_base_url": settings.ollama_base_url,
        "openrouter_configured": bool(settings.openrouter_api_key),
        "supported_providers": list(SUPPORTED_PROVIDERS),
    }


@router.get("/api/tools")
async def list_available_tools() -> dict[str, Any]:
    tools = tool_registry.list_tools()
    return {"status": "ok", "count": len(tools), "tools": tools}


@router.post("/api/tools/{tool_name}/run")
async def run_tool_directly(tool_name: str, payload: ToolRunRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not tool_registry.get(tool_name):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Tool '{tool_name}' not found.")
    result = await tool_registry.execute(tool_name, payload.arguments)
    return {"status": "ok", "tool": tool_name, "result": result}


@router.get("/chat/sessions", response_model=SessionListResponse)
async def list_chat_sessions() -> SessionListResponse:
    raw_list = memory_service.list_sessions()
    summaries = [SessionSummary(**item) for item in raw_list]
    return SessionListResponse(sessions=summaries)


@router.post("/chat/sessions")
async def create_new_session() -> dict[str, str]:
    session_id = memory_service.create_session_id()
    memory_service.ensure_session(session_id, title="New Chat")
    return {"status": "ok", "session_id": session_id, "title": "New Chat"}


@router.delete("/chat/sessions/{session_id}")
async def delete_chat_session(session_id: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    cleaned_id = session_id.strip()
    if not cleaned_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Session ID cannot be empty.")
    memory_service.clear_session(cleaned_id)
    return {"status": "ok", "session_id": cleaned_id}


@router.get("/api/ollama/models")
async def discover_ollama_models(
    base_url: str | None = Query(default=None),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    settings = get_settings()
    target_url = (base_url or settings.ollama_base_url).strip().rstrip("/")
    provider = OllamaProvider(base_url=target_url)

    try:
        models = await provider.list_models()
    except LLMProviderError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=exc.status_code, detail=exc.message) from exc

    return {
        "status": "ok",
        "base_url": target_url,
        "count": len(models),
        "models": models,
    }


@router.get("/api/ollama/health")
async def check_ollama_health(
    base_url: str | None = Query(default=None),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    settings = get_settings()
    target_url = (base_url or settings.ollama_base_url).strip().rstrip("/")
    provider = OllamaProvider(base_url=target_url)

    try:
        models = await provider.list_models()
    except LLMProviderError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=exc.status_code, detail=exc.message) from exc

    return {
        "status": "online",
        "base_url": target_url,
        "installed_models": len(models),
    }


@router.get("/chat/pinned-model")
async def get_pinned_model() -> dict[str, str | None]:
    return {
        "pinned_model": _pinned_free_model,
        "pinned_vision_model": _pinned_vision_model,
        "pinned_tool_model": _pinned_tool_model,
    }


@router.post("/chat/reset-model")
async def reset_pinned_model() -> dict[str, str | None]:
    global _pinned_free_model, _pinned_vision_model, _pinned_tool_model
    _pinned_free_model = None
    _pinned_vision_model = None
    _pinned_tool_model = None
    return {
        "status": "ok",
        "pinned_model": None,
        "pinned_vision_model": None,
        "pinned_tool_model": None,
    }


@router.get("/api/a2ui/showcase")
async def get_a2ui_showcase() -> dict[str, Any]:
    output = a2ui_service.get_showcase_output()
    return {
        "status": "ok",
        "components_supported": list(ALL_BASIC_COMPONENTS),
        "component_count": len(ALL_BASIC_COMPONENTS),
        "output": output.model_dump(),
    }


@router.get("/chat/history/{session_id}", response_model=ChatHistoryResponse)
async def get_chat_history(session_id: str) -> ChatHistoryResponse:
    cleaned_id = session_id.strip()
    if not cleaned_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Session ID cannot be empty.")
    info = memory_service.get_session_info(cleaned_id)
    title = info["title"] if info else "Chat"
    return ChatHistoryResponse(
        session_id=cleaned_id,
        title=title,
        messages=memory_service.get_history(cleaned_id),
    )


@router.delete("/chat/history/{session_id}")
async def clear_chat_history(session_id: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    cleaned_id = session_id.strip()
    if not cleaned_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Session ID cannot be empty.")
    memory_service.clear_session(cleaned_id)
    return {"status": "ok", "session_id": cleaned_id}


async def _parse_with_optional_repair(
    provider: LLMProvider,
    initial_result: LLMResponse,
    base_history: list[dict[str, Any]],
    user_prompt: str,
    ui_mode: str,
) -> tuple[StandardizedLLMOutput, LLMResponse]:
    standardized = a2ui_service.parse_and_standardize(
        initial_result.content,
        ui_mode=ui_mode,
    )

    if ui_mode == "text" or standardized.used_a2ui or not standardized.validation_warnings:
        return standardized, initial_result

    repair_messages = [
        *base_history,
        {"role": "user", "content": user_prompt},
        {"role": "assistant", "content": initial_result.content},
        {"role": "user", "content": a2ui_service.build_repair_prompt(standardized.validation_warnings)},
    ]

    try:
        repaired_result = await provider.chat(
            messages=repair_messages,
            model=initial_result.model,
        )
    except LLMProviderError:
        return standardized, initial_result

    repaired_standardized = a2ui_service.parse_and_standardize(
        repaired_result.content,
        ui_mode=ui_mode,
    )
    repaired_standardized = repaired_standardized.model_copy(update={"repair_retried": True})

    if repaired_standardized.used_a2ui:
        return repaired_standardized, repaired_result

    combined_warnings = [
        *standardized.validation_warnings,
        *repaired_standardized.validation_warnings,
    ]
    return (
        repaired_standardized.model_copy(update={"validation_warnings": combined_warnings}),
        repaired_result,
    )


@router.post("/chat", response_model=ChatResponse)
async def chat(payload: ChatRequest) -> ChatResponse:
    global _pinned_free_model, _pinned_vision_model, _pinned_tool_model

    settings = get_settings()
    session_id = (payload.session_id or "").strip() or memory_service.create_session_id()
    memory_service.ensure_session(session_id)

    try:
        active_provider, provider = create_llm_provider(
            settings=settings,
            provider_name=payload.provider,
            api_key=payload.api_key,
            default_model=payload.model,
            ollama_base_url=payload.ollama_base_url,
        )
    except LLMProviderError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=exc.status_code, detail=exc.message) from exc

    if active_provider == "openrouter":
        requested_model = (payload.model or "").strip() or settings.model_name or FREE_ROUTER_MODEL
    else:
        env_ollama_model = "" if settings.model_name == FREE_ROUTER_MODEL else settings.model_name
        requested_model = (payload.model or "").strip() or env_ollama_model

    is_vision = bool(payload.image_id)
    image_data_url: str | None = None

    if payload.image_id:
        try:
            image_data_url = image_service.load_image_as_data_url(payload.image_id)
        except ImageValidationError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=exc.status_code, detail=exc.message) from exc

    if payload.document_id:
        try:
            doc_text, _, _ = file_service.extract_text_by_id(payload.document_id)
        except FileValidationError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=exc.status_code, detail=exc.message) from exc

        doc_display_name = payload.document_name or payload.document_id
        memory_service.attach_document(
            session_id=session_id,
            file_id=payload.document_id,
            filename=doc_display_name,
            text=doc_text,
        )

    prior_messages = memory_service.get_history(session_id)
    if active_provider == "ollama":
        prior_messages = prior_messages[-4:]

    documents_context = memory_service.get_documents_context(session_id)
    has_document_context = bool(documents_context)

    use_tools = bool(payload.enable_tools and not is_vision and active_provider == "openrouter")

    a2ui_system_prompt = a2ui_service.build_system_prompt(
        payload.ui_mode,
        provider=active_provider,
    )

    base_history: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    if a2ui_system_prompt:
        base_history.append({"role": "system", "content": a2ui_system_prompt})
    if use_tools:
        base_history.append(
            {
                "role": "system",
                "content": (
                    "You have access to backend tools on the Oracle VM (VM status, SQLite chat DB search, "
                    "uploaded files/documents, and a local Ollama worker). Call a tool only when needed to "
                    "answer accurately."
                ),
            }
        )
    base_history.extend(prior_messages)

    async def run_inference(target_model: str | None) -> AgentResult:
        if is_vision and image_data_url is not None:
            vision_history = list(base_history)
            if documents_context:
                vision_history.append(
                    {
                        "role": "user",
                        "content": f"Uploaded document context:\n\n{documents_context}",
                    }
                )
            resp = await provider.analyze_image(
                prompt=payload.message,
                image_data_url=image_data_url,
                history=vision_history,
                model=target_model,
            )
            return AgentResult(response=resp, tool_traces=[])

        if documents_context:
            if use_tools:
                doc_messages = [
                    *base_history,
                    {
                        "role": "user",
                        "content": (
                            "Use the following uploaded document content to answer the user's questions accurately.\n\n"
                            f"{documents_context}"
                        ),
                    },
                    {"role": "user", "content": payload.message},
                ]
                return await agent_service.run(
                    provider=provider,
                    messages=doc_messages,
                    model=target_model,
                    enable_tools=True,
                )
            resp = await provider.process_document(
                prompt=payload.message,
                document_context=documents_context,
                history=base_history,
                model=target_model,
            )
            return AgentResult(response=resp, tool_traces=[])

        if use_tools:
            return await agent_service.run(
                provider=provider,
                messages=[*base_history, {"role": "user", "content": payload.message}],
                model=target_model,
                enable_tools=True,
            )

        resp = await provider.chat(
            messages=[*base_history, {"role": "user", "content": payload.message}],
            model=target_model,
        )
        return AgentResult(response=resp, tool_traces=[])

    memory_prefixes: list[str] = []
    if payload.image_id:
        memory_prefixes.append(f"[Uploaded image: {payload.image_id}]")
    if payload.document_id:
        memory_prefixes.append(
            f"[Uploaded document: {payload.document_name or payload.document_id}]"
        )
    memory_user_text = (
        f"{' '.join(memory_prefixes)} {payload.message}"
        if memory_prefixes
        else payload.message
    )

    if active_provider == "openrouter" and requested_model == FREE_ROUTER_MODEL:
        if is_vision:
            active_pinned = (payload.pinned_vision_model or "").strip() or _pinned_vision_model
        elif use_tools:
            active_pinned = (payload.pinned_tool_model or "").strip() or _pinned_tool_model
        else:
            active_pinned = (payload.pinned_model or "").strip() or _pinned_free_model

        target_model = active_pinned or FREE_ROUTER_MODEL

        try:
            agent_result = await run_inference(target_model)
        except LLMProviderError as exc:
            if target_model != FREE_ROUTER_MODEL and exc.status_code in RETRIABLE_PIN_STATUSES:
                if is_vision:
                    _pinned_vision_model = None
                elif use_tools:
                    _pinned_tool_model = None
                else:
                    _pinned_free_model = None
                try:
                    agent_result = await run_inference(FREE_ROUTER_MODEL)
                except LLMProviderError as retry_exc:
                    raise HTTPException(
                        status_code=retry_exc.status_code,
                        detail=retry_exc.message,
                    ) from retry_exc
            else:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=exc.status_code, detail=exc.message) from exc

        result = agent_result.response
        if result.model and result.model != FREE_ROUTER_MODEL:
            if is_vision:
                _pinned_vision_model = result.model
            elif use_tools:
                _pinned_tool_model = result.model
            else:
                _pinned_free_model = result.model

        standardized, final_result = await _parse_with_optional_repair(
            provider=provider,
            initial_result=result,
            base_history=base_history,
            user_prompt=payload.message,
            ui_mode=payload.ui_mode,
        )

        updated_history = memory_service.append_turn(
            session_id=session_id,
            user_message=memory_user_text,
            assistant_reply=standardized.text,
        )

        pin_flag = bool(
            _pinned_vision_model
            if is_vision
            else (_pinned_tool_model if use_tools else _pinned_free_model)
        )
        return ChatResponse(
            reply=standardized.text,
            output=standardized,
            session_id=session_id,
            history_length=len(updated_history),
            provider=active_provider,
            model=final_result.model,
            pinned=pin_flag,
            vision_used=is_vision,
            document_used=has_document_context,
            tools_used=bool(agent_result.tool_traces),
            tool_traces=[ToolTrace(**t) for t in agent_result.tool_traces],
        )

    try:
        agent_result = await run_inference(requested_model or None)
    except LLMProviderError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=exc.status_code, detail=exc.message) from exc

    standardized, final_result = await _parse_with_optional_repair(
        provider=provider,
        initial_result=agent_result.response,
        base_history=base_history,
        user_prompt=payload.message,
        ui_mode=payload.ui_mode,
    )

    updated_history = memory_service.append_turn(
        session_id=session_id,
        user_message=memory_user_text,
        assistant_reply=standardized.text,
    )

    return ChatResponse(
        reply=standardized.text,
        output=standardized,
        session_id=session_id,
        history_length=len(updated_history),
        provider=active_provider,
        model=final_result.model,
        pinned=False,
        vision_used=is_vision,
        document_used=has_document_context,
        tools_used=bool(agent_result.tool_traces),
        tool_traces=[ToolTrace(**t) for t in agent_result.tool_traces],
    )

=== FILE: backend/routes/upload.py ===
from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import APIRouter, File, HTTPException, UploadFile

from backend.models import DocumentUploadResponse, ImageUploadResponse
from backend.services.file_service import FileService, FileValidationError
from backend.services.image_service import ImageService, ImageValidationError

router = APIRouter(tags=["upload"])

UPLOADS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent / "uploads"
image_service = ImageService(uploads_dir=UPLOADS_DIR)
file_service = FileService(uploads_dir=UPLOADS_DIR)


@router.post("/upload/image", response_model=ImageUploadResponse)
async def upload_image(file: UploadFile = File(...)) -> ImageUploadResponse:
    try:
        saved = await image_service.save_image(file)
    except ImageValidationError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=exc.status_code, detail=exc.message) from exc

    return ImageUploadResponse(
        status="ok",
        file_id=saved.file_id,
        original_filename=saved.original_filename,
        url=saved.url,
        content_type=saved.content_type,
        size_bytes=saved.size_bytes,
        width=saved.width,
        height=saved.height,
    )


@router.post("/upload/document", response_model=DocumentUploadResponse)
async def upload_document(file: UploadFile = File(...)) -> DocumentUploadResponse:
    try:
        saved = await file_service.save_document(file)
    except FileValidationError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=exc.status_code, detail=exc.message) from exc

    preview_text = saved.extracted_text[:200].replace("\n", " ").strip()
    if len(saved.extracted_text) > 200:
        preview_text += "..."

    return DocumentUploadResponse(
        status="ok",
        file_id=saved.file_id,
        original_filename=saved.original_filename,
        file_type=saved.file_type,
        size_bytes=saved.size_bytes,
        page_count=saved.page_count,
        char_count=saved.char_count,
        truncated=saved.truncated,
        preview=preview_text,
    )

=== FILE: backend/services/a2ui_service.py ===
import re
from typing import Any

from a2ui.core.basic_catalog import BasicCatalog
from a2ui.core.schema import ProtocolVersion
from a2ui.inference_formats import ExpressFormatFactory
from a2ui.parser import A2uiPart, TextPart, to_message_dicts
from a2ui.processor import A2uiGenerator, CatalogConfig
from a2ui.utils import validate_payload

from backend.models import StandardizedLLMOutput

A2UI_BLOCK_PATTERN = re.compile(r"<a2ui>.*?</a2ui>", re.DOTALL | re.IGNORECASE)
ALL_BASIC_COMPONENTS = (
    "Row",
    "Column",
    "List",
    "Card",
    "Tabs",
    "Modal",
    "Divider",
    "Text",
    "Image",
    "Icon",
    "Video",
    "AudioPlayer",
    "Button",
    "TextField",
    "CheckBox",
    "ChoicePicker",
    "Slider",
    "DateTimeInput",
)

STRICT_EXPRESS_RULES = """STRICT A2UI EXPRESS RULES:
1. Always start inside <a2ui>...</a2ui> with: root = Card(...) or root = Column(...)
2. NEVER invent custom surface names (e.g. do NOT write "demo" or createSurface("name")).
3. NEVER use slash commands (e.g. /updateComponents) or raw JSON inside <a2ui>...</a2ui>.
4. All string literals must be quoted ("..."), and Text.variant may only be "body" or "caption".
5. Always provide a concise plain-text reply outside <a2ui>...</a2ui> tags."""

MINIMAL_EXPRESS_EXAMPLE = """Minimal valid example:
Here is a quick option card.
<a2ui>
root = Card(Column([
  Text("Quick Summary", variant="body"),
  Button(Text("Confirm"), variant="primary", action=Event("confirm_action"))
]))
</a2ui>"""


class A2uiService:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.catalog = BasicCatalog(ProtocolVersion.V1_0)
        self.generator = A2uiGenerator(
            [CatalogConfig(self.catalog)],
            inference_format_factory=ExpressFormatFactory(),
        )
        self.capabilities = {
            "1.0": {
                "supportedCatalogIds": [self.catalog.catalog_id],
            }
        }
        self._base_processor = self.generator.create_processor(self.capabilities)

    def build_system_prompt(self, ui_mode: str, provider: str = "openrouter") -> str | None:
        if ui_mode == "text":
            return None

        # PERFORMANCE: When running local models on CPU, do not inject the 5,000-token
        # A2UI grammar in auto mode. Keep it lean so the prompt processes in 1-2 seconds.
        if provider == "ollama" and ui_mode != "a2ui":
            return (
                "You are ORACLE CHAT, a helpful assistant running locally on Oracle Cloud. "
                "Answer questions directly, accurately, and concisely."
            )

        if ui_mode == "a2ui":
            mode_rule = (
                "ALWAYS include a valid <a2ui>...</a2ui> Express UI block along with a brief 1-2 sentence "
                "plain-text summary outside the tags.\n\n"
                f"{MINIMAL_EXPRESS_EXAMPLE}"
            )
        else:
            mode_rule = (
                "Be CONSERVATIVE with <a2ui>: reply in plain text ONLY for normal conversation, Q&A, "
                "explanations, and document summaries. ONLY include <a2ui>...</a2ui> when the user "
                "explicitly asks for a form, interactive card, checklist, choices, slider, or UI widget."
            )

        return (
            "You are ORACLE CHAT, a helpful assistant capable of generating interactive A2UI v1.0 interfaces.\n"
            f"{mode_rule}\n\n"
            f"{STRICT_EXPRESS_RULES}\n\n"
            f"{self._base_processor.prompt_snippet}"
        )

    @staticmethod
    def build_repair_prompt(warnings: list[str]) -> str:
        error_summary = "; ".join(warnings)
        return (
            "Your previous <a2ui>...</a2ui> block failed A2UI Express validation with:\n"
            f"{error_summary}\n\n"
            "Rewrite your response with a valid <a2ui>...</a2ui> block following these exact rules:\n"
            "- Start with: root = Card(...) or root = Column(...)\n"
            "- Do NOT invent custom surface names or slash commands.\n"
            "- Text.variant can only be \"body\" or \"caption\".\n"
            "- Keep a brief plain-text summary outside <a2ui>...</a2ui>."
        )

    def parse_and_standardize(self, raw_reply: str, ui_mode: str = "auto") -> StandardizedLLMOutput:
        cleaned_raw = (raw_reply or "").strip()
        if ui_mode == "text":
            return StandardizedLLMOutput(
                text=cleaned_raw,
                a2ui_messages=[],
                used_a2ui=False,
                ui_mode="text",
                raw_format="text",
            )

        processor = self.generator.create_processor(self.capabilities)
        warnings: list[str] = []

        try:
            parts = processor.parse_response(cleaned_raw)
        except Exception as exc:
            warnings.append(f"A2UI parse/validation fallback: {exc}")
            fallback_text = A2UI_BLOCK_PATTERN.sub("", cleaned_raw).strip() or cleaned_raw
            return StandardizedLLMOutput(
                text=fallback_text,
                a2ui_messages=[],
                used_a2ui=False,
                ui_mode=ui_mode,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                raw_format="text",
                validation_warnings=warnings,
            )

        text_segments: list[str] = []
        wire_messages: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

        for part in parts:
            if isinstance(part, TextPart):
                if part.text and part.text.strip():
                    text_segments.append(part.text.strip())
            elif isinstance(part, A2uiPart):
                wire_messages.extend(to_message_dicts(part.a2ui))

        if wire_messages:
            try:
                validate_payload([self.catalog], wire_messages)
            except Exception as exc:
                warnings.append(f"A2UI catalog validation fallback: {exc}")
                fallback_text = (
                    "\n\n".join(text_segments).strip()
                    or A2UI_BLOCK_PATTERN.sub("", cleaned_raw).strip()
                    or cleaned_raw
                )
                return StandardizedLLMOutput(
                    text=fallback_text,
                    a2ui_messages=[],
                    used_a2ui=False,
                    ui_mode=ui_mode,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                    raw_format="text",
                    validation_warnings=warnings,
                )

        final_text = "\n\n".join(text_segments).strip()
        if not final_text:
            final_text = "Interactive A2UI surface rendered below." if wire_messages else cleaned_raw

        return StandardizedLLMOutput(
            text=final_text,
            a2ui_messages=wire_messages,
            used_a2ui=bool(wire_messages),
            ui_mode=ui_mode,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            raw_format="express" if wire_messages else "text",
            validation_warnings=warnings,
        )

=== FILE: backend/services/agent_service.py ===
import json
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

from backend.llm.base import LLMProvider, LLMResponse
from backend.tools.registry import ToolRegistry


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AgentResult:
    response: LLMResponse
    tool_traces: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)


class AgentService:
    def __init__(self, tool_registry: ToolRegistry, max_steps: int = 5) -> None:
        self.tool_registry = tool_registry
        self.max_steps = max_steps

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_tool_arguments(raw_args: Any) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], str | None]:
        if raw_args is None or raw_args == "":
            return {}, None
        if isinstance(raw_args, dict):
            return raw_args, None
        if isinstance(raw_args, str):
            try:
                parsed = json.loads(raw_args)
                if isinstance(parsed, dict):
                    return parsed, None
                return {}, f"Tool arguments must be a JSON object, got {type(parsed).__name__}"
            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
                return {}, f"Invalid JSON tool arguments: {exc}"
        return {}, f"Unsupported argument type: {type(raw_args).__name__}"

    async def run(
        self,
        provider: LLMProvider,
        messages: list[dict[str, Any]],
        model: str | None = None,
        enable_tools: bool = True,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        tool_schemas = self.tool_registry.openai_schemas() if enable_tools else None
        working_messages = [dict(m) for m in messages]
        traces: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        last_response: LLMResponse | None = None

        for step in range(1, self.max_steps + 1):
            last_response = await provider.chat(
                messages=working_messages,
                model=model,
                tools=tool_schemas,
            )

            if not last_response.tool_calls:
                return AgentResult(response=last_response, tool_traces=traces)

            assistant_tool_msg: dict[str, Any] = {
                "role": "assistant",
                "content": last_response.content or "",
                "tool_calls": last_response.tool_calls,
            }
            working_messages.append(assistant_tool_msg)

            for idx, tc in enumerate(last_response.tool_calls):
                call_id = str(tc.get("id") or f"call_{step}_{idx}")
                fn_obj = tc.get("function") or {}
                tool_name = str(fn_obj.get("name") or tc.get("name") or "").strip()
                raw_args = fn_obj.get("arguments", tc.get("arguments"))

                parsed_args, parse_err = self._parse_tool_arguments(raw_args)
                started = time.perf_counter()
                if parse_err:
                    result_obj: dict[str, Any] = {"error": parse_err}
                else:
                    result_obj = await self.tool_registry.execute(tool_name, parsed_args)
                duration_ms = round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000, 1)

                serialized_result = json.dumps(result_obj, default=str)
                traces.append(
                    {
                        "step": step,
                        "tool": tool_name,
                        "arguments": parsed_args,
                        "duration_ms": duration_ms,
                        "ok": "error" not in result_obj,
                        "result_preview": serialized_result[:400],
                    }
                )

                working_messages.append(
                    {
                        "role": "tool",
                        "tool_call_id": call_id,
                        "name": tool_name,
                        "content": serialized_result,
                    }
                )

        final_response = await provider.chat(
            messages=working_messages,
            model=last_response.model if last_response else model,
            tools=None,
        )
        return AgentResult(response=final_response, tool_traces=traces)

=== END FILE ===

=== FILE: backend/services/file_service.py ===
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from uuid import uuid4

from fastapi import UploadFile
from pypdf import PdfReader

ALLOWED_DOC_EXTENSIONS = {".pdf", ".txt"}
MAX_DOCUMENT_BYTES = 15 * 1024 * 1024  # 15 MB
MAX_EXTRACTED_CHARS = 20000


class FileValidationError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, message: str, status_code: int = 400) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.message = message
        self.status_code = status_code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SavedDocumentInfo:
    file_id: str
    original_filename: str
    file_type: str
    size_bytes: int
    page_count: int
    char_count: int
    truncated: bool
    extracted_text: str


class FileService:
    def __init__(self, uploads_dir: Path) -> None:
        self.uploads_dir = uploads_dir
        self.uploads_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    async def save_document(self, file: UploadFile) -> SavedDocumentInfo:
        original_name = (file.filename or "document").strip()
        suffix = Path(original_name).suffix.lower()

        if suffix not in ALLOWED_DOC_EXTENSIONS:
            allowed_list = ", ".join(sorted(ALLOWED_DOC_EXTENSIONS))
            raise FileValidationError(
                f"Unsupported document extension '{suffix or 'none'}'. Allowed: {allowed_list}",
                status_code=400,
            )

        content = await file.read()
        if not content:
            raise FileValidationError("Uploaded document file is empty.", status_code=400)

        if len(content) > MAX_DOCUMENT_BYTES:
            raise FileValidationError(
                f"Document exceeds maximum allowed size of {MAX_DOCUMENT_BYTES // (1024 * 1024)} MB.",
                status_code=413,
            )

        saved_filename = f"{uuid4().hex}{suffix}"
        destination = self.uploads_dir / saved_filename
        destination.write_bytes(content)

        try:
            extracted_text, page_count = self._extract_from_path(destination, suffix)
        except FileValidationError:
            destination.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            raise

        truncated = False
        if len(extracted_text) > MAX_EXTRACTED_CHARS:
            extracted_text = (
                extracted_text[:MAX_EXTRACTED_CHARS].rstrip()
                + f"\n\n[Note: Document text truncated at {MAX_EXTRACTED_CHARS:,} characters.]"
            )
            truncated = True

        return SavedDocumentInfo(
            file_id=saved_filename,
            original_filename=original_name,
            file_type=suffix.lstrip("."),
            size_bytes=len(content),
            page_count=page_count,
            char_count=len(extracted_text),
            truncated=truncated,
            extracted_text=extracted_text,
        )

    def extract_text_by_id(self, file_id: str) -> tuple[str, int, bool]:
        cleaned_id = file_id.strip()
        if not cleaned_id or Path(cleaned_id).name != cleaned_id:
            raise FileValidationError("Invalid document file_id.", status_code=400)

        doc_path = self.uploads_dir / cleaned_id
        if not doc_path.is_file():
            raise FileValidationError(
                f"Uploaded document '{cleaned_id}' was not found.",
                status_code=404,
            )

        suffix = doc_path.suffix.lower()
        if suffix not in ALLOWED_DOC_EXTENSIONS:
            raise FileValidationError(
                f"Unsupported document extension '{suffix}'.",
                status_code=400,
            )

        text, page_count = self._extract_from_path(doc_path, suffix)
        truncated = False
        if len(text) > MAX_EXTRACTED_CHARS:
            text = (
                text[:MAX_EXTRACTED_CHARS].rstrip()
                + f"\n\n[Note: Document text truncated at {MAX_EXTRACTED_CHARS:,} characters.]"
            )
            truncated = True

        return text, page_count, truncated

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_from_path(file_path: Path, suffix: str) -> tuple[str, int]:
        if suffix == ".txt":
            raw_bytes = file_path.read_bytes()
            text = ""
            for encoding in ("utf-8", "utf-8-sig", "latin-1"):
                try:
                    text = raw_bytes.decode(encoding)
                    break
                except UnicodeDecodeError:
                    continue
            cleaned = text.strip()
            if not cleaned:
                raise FileValidationError("Text file contains no readable text.", status_code=400)
            return cleaned, 1

        try:
            reader = PdfReader(str(file_path))
        except Exception as exc:
            raise FileValidationError(
                f"Failed to read PDF file: {exc}",
                status_code=400,
            ) from exc

        pages_text: list[str] = []
        for idx, page in enumerate(reader.pages, start=1):
            page_content = (page.extract_text() or "").strip()
            if page_content:
                pages_text.append(f"[Page {idx}]\n{page_content}")

        combined = "\n\n".join(pages_text).strip()
        if not combined:
            raise FileValidationError(
                "No extractable text found in this PDF (it may be a scanned/image-only PDF).",
                status_code=400,
            )

        return combined, len(reader.pages)

=== END FILE ===

=== FILE: backend/services/image_service.py ===
import base64
import io
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from uuid import uuid4

from fastapi import UploadFile
from PIL import Image, UnidentifiedImageError

ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS = {".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp"}
ALLOWED_PIL_FORMATS = {"JPEG", "PNG", "WEBP"}
EXTENSION_TO_MIME = {
    ".jpg": "image/jpeg",
    ".jpeg": "image/jpeg",
    ".png": "image/png",
    ".webp": "image/webp",
}
MAX_IMAGE_BYTES = 10 * 1024 * 1024  # 10 MB


class ImageValidationError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, message: str, status_code: int = 400) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.message = message
        self.status_code = status_code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SavedImageInfo:
    file_id: str
    original_filename: str
    url: str
    content_type: str
    size_bytes: int
    width: int
    height: int


class ImageService:
    def __init__(self, uploads_dir: Path) -> None:
        self.uploads_dir = uploads_dir
        self.uploads_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    async def save_image(self, file: UploadFile) -> SavedImageInfo:
        original_name = (file.filename or "image").strip()
        suffix = Path(original_name).suffix.lower()

        if suffix not in ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS:
            allowed_list = ", ".join(sorted(ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS))
            raise ImageValidationError(
                f"Unsupported image extension '{suffix or 'none'}'. Allowed: {allowed_list}",
                status_code=400,
            )

        content = await file.read()
        if not content:
            raise ImageValidationError("Uploaded image file is empty.", status_code=400)

        if len(content) > MAX_IMAGE_BYTES:
            raise ImageValidationError(
                f"Image exceeds maximum allowed size of {MAX_IMAGE_BYTES // (1024 * 1024)} MB.",
                status_code=413,
            )

        try:
            with Image.open(io.BytesIO(content)) as img:
                img.verify()
            with Image.open(io.BytesIO(content)) as img:
                width, height = img.size
                pil_format = (img.format or "").upper()
        except (UnidentifiedImageError, OSError, ValueError) as exc:
            raise ImageValidationError(
                "Invalid or corrupted image file. Please upload a valid JPG, PNG, or WEBP image.",
                status_code=400,
            ) from exc

        if pil_format not in ALLOWED_PIL_FORMATS:
            raise ImageValidationError(
                f"Unsupported image format '{pil_format}'. Allowed: JPEG, PNG, WEBP.",
                status_code=400,
            )

        normalized_ext = ".jpg" if pil_format == "JPEG" else f".{pil_format.lower()}"
        saved_filename = f"{uuid4().hex}{normalized_ext}"
        destination = self.uploads_dir / saved_filename
        destination.write_bytes(content)

        content_type = file.content_type or EXTENSION_TO_MIME[normalized_ext]
        return SavedImageInfo(
            file_id=saved_filename,
            original_filename=original_name,
            url=f"/uploads/{saved_filename}",
            content_type=content_type,
            size_bytes=len(content),
            width=width,
            height=height,
        )

    def load_image_as_data_url(self, file_id: str) -> str:
        cleaned_id = file_id.strip()
        if not cleaned_id or Path(cleaned_id).name != cleaned_id:
            raise ImageValidationError("Invalid image file_id.", status_code=400)

        image_path = self.uploads_dir / cleaned_id
        if not image_path.is_file():
            raise ImageValidationError(
                f"Uploaded image '{cleaned_id}' was not found.",
                status_code=404,
            )

        suffix = image_path.suffix.lower()
        mime_type = EXTENSION_TO_MIME.get(suffix)
        if not mime_type:
            raise ImageValidationError(
                f"Unsupported image extension '{suffix}'.",
                status_code=400,
            )

        encoded = base64.b64encode(image_path.read_bytes()).decode("ascii")
        return f"data:{mime_type};base64,{encoded}"

=== END FILE ===

=== FILE: backend/services/memory_service.py ===
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any
from uuid import uuid4

DEFAULT_DB_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent / "data" / "oracle_chat.db"


def utc_now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


class MemoryService:
    def __init__(
        self,
        db_path: Path | str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH,
        max_messages: int = 40,
        max_doc_chars: int = 24000,
    ) -> None:
        self.db_path = Path(db_path)
        self.db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.max_messages = max_messages
        self.max_doc_chars = max_doc_chars
        self._init_db()

    def _get_connection(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))
        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;")
        return conn

    def _init_db(self) -> None:
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            conn.executescript(
                """
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sessions (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    title TEXT NOT NULL,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );

                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                    session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    role TEXT NOT NULL,
                    content TEXT NOT NULL,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES sessions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
                );

                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS documents (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                    session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    file_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    filename TEXT NOT NULL,
                    text TEXT NOT NULL,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES sessions (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
                );

                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_messages_session ON messages(session_id);
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_documents_session ON documents(session_id);
                """
            )

    @staticmethod
    def create_session_id() -> str:
        return uuid4().hex

    def ensure_session(self, session_id: str, title: str = "New Chat") -> str:
        cleaned_id = session_id.strip() or self.create_session_id()
        now = utc_now_iso()
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT OR IGNORE INTO sessions (id, title, created_at, updated_at)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
                """,
                (cleaned_id, title, now, now),
            )
        return cleaned_id

    def list_sessions(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            rows = conn.execute(
                """
                SELECT s.id, s.title, s.created_at, s.updated_at,
                       COUNT(m.id) AS message_count
                FROM sessions s
                LEFT JOIN messages m ON s.id = m.session_id
                GROUP BY s.id
                ORDER BY s.updated_at DESC
                """
            ).fetchall()

            return [
                {
                    "id": row["id"],
                    "title": row["title"],
                    "created_at": row["created_at"],
                    "updated_at": row["updated_at"],
                    "message_count": row["message_count"],
                }
                for row in rows
            ]

    def get_session_info(self, session_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            row = conn.execute(
                "SELECT id, title, created_at, updated_at FROM sessions WHERE id = ?",
                (session_id,),
            ).fetchone()
            if not row:
                return None
            return dict(row)

    def get_history(self, session_id: str, limit: int | None = None) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
        effective_limit = limit or self.max_messages
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            rows = conn.execute(
                """
                SELECT role, content FROM (
                    SELECT id, role, content FROM messages
                    WHERE session_id = ?
                    ORDER BY id DESC
                    LIMIT ?
                ) ORDER BY id ASC
                """,
                (session_id, effective_limit),
            ).fetchall()

            return [{"role": row["role"], "content": row["content"]} for row in rows]

    def append_turn(
        self,
        session_id: str,
        user_message: str,
        assistant_reply: str,
    ) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
        self.ensure_session(session_id)
        now = utc_now_iso()

        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO messages (session_id, role, content, created_at) VALUES (?, 'user', ?, ?)",
                (session_id, user_message, now),
            )
            conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO messages (session_id, role, content, created_at) VALUES (?, 'assistant', ?, ?)",
                (session_id, assistant_reply, now),
            )

            # Auto-title session from the first user message if still default
            current = conn.execute(
                "SELECT title FROM sessions WHERE id = ?", (session_id,)
            ).fetchone()
            if current and current["title"] in ("New Chat", "Chat"):
                clean_title = user_message.strip()
                if clean_title.startswith("[Uploaded"):
                    clean_title = clean_title.split("]", 1)[-1].strip() or clean_title
                snippet = (clean_title[:38] + "…") if len(clean_title) > 40 else clean_title
                conn.execute(
                    "UPDATE sessions SET title = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
                    (snippet or "New Chat", now, session_id),
                )
            else:
                conn.execute(
                    "UPDATE sessions SET updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
                    (now, session_id),
                )

        return self.get_history(session_id)

    def attach_document(
        self,
        session_id: str,
        file_id: str,
        filename: str,
        text: str,
    ) -> None:
        self.ensure_session(session_id)
        now = utc_now_iso()
        trimmed_text = text[: self.max_doc_chars]

        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            conn.execute(
                "DELETE FROM documents WHERE session_id = ? AND file_id = ?",
                (session_id, file_id),
            )
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO documents (session_id, file_id, filename, text, created_at)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """,
                (session_id, file_id, filename, trimmed_text, now),
            )
            conn.execute(
                "UPDATE sessions SET updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
                (now, session_id),
            )

    def get_documents_context(self, session_id: str) -> str:
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            rows = conn.execute(
                """
                SELECT filename, text FROM documents
                WHERE session_id = ?
                ORDER BY id DESC
                LIMIT 2
                """,
                (session_id,),
            ).fetchall()

            if not rows:
                return ""

            sections = [
                f"Document: {row['filename']}\n--- BEGIN DOCUMENT ---\n{row['text']}\n--- END DOCUMENT ---"
                for row in reversed(rows)
            ]
            return "\n\n".join(sections)

    def clear_session(self, session_id: str) -> None:
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            conn.execute("DELETE FROM sessions WHERE id = ?", (session_id,))

=== FILE: bot/main.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import asyncio
import os
import sys
import logging
import httpx
from pathlib import Path
from dotenv import load_dotenv

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
load_dotenv(BASE_DIR / ".env")

TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN = os.getenv("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN", "").strip()
OWNER_TELEGRAM_ID = os.getenv("OWNER_TELEGRAM_ID", "").strip()
OLLAMA_MODEL = os.getenv("OLLAMA_MODEL", "qwen3.5:9b").strip()
OLLAMA_API_URL = os.getenv("OLLAMA_API_URL", "http://localhost:11434/api/chat").strip()

logging.basicConfig(format="%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s", level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

TELEGRAM_API = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN}"

async def query_ollama(prompt: str) -> str:
    logger.info(f"Querying Ollama model: {OLLAMA_MODEL} with prompt: {prompt[:50]}...")
    payload = {
        "model": OLLAMA_MODEL,
        "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
        "stream": False,
        "options": {"num_ctx": 4096}
    }
    try:
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=300.0) as client:
            response = await client.post(OLLAMA_API_URL, json=payload)
            if response.status_code != 200:
                return f"Ollama error (HTTP {response.status_code}): {response.text}"
            data = response.json()
            return data.get("message", {}).get("content", "Empty response.")
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Failed to connect to Ollama")
        return f"Failed to reach local Ollama engine: {exc}"

async def send_telegram_message(chat_id: str, text: str):
    url = f"{TELEGRAM_API}/sendMessage"
    payload = {"chat_id": chat_id, "text": text}
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=10.0) as client:
        await client.post(url, json=payload)

async def poll_telegram():
    logger.info("Bot polling started.")
    offset = 0
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=35.0) as client:
        while True:
            try:
                resp = await client.get(f"{TELEGRAM_API}/getUpdates?offset={offset}&timeout=30")
                if resp.status_code != 200:
                    await asyncio.sleep(5); continue
                
                for result in resp.json().get("result", []):
                    offset = result["update_id"] + 1
                    msg = result.get("message", {})
                    
                    user_id = str(msg.get("from", {}).get("id", ""))
                    chat_id = str(msg.get("chat", {}).get("id", ""))
                    text = msg.get("text", "").strip()
                    
                    if user_id != OWNER_TELEGRAM_ID:
                        logger.warning(f"Unauthorized: {user_id}")
                        continue
                    
                    if not text:
                        logger.info("Ignoring non-text message")
                        continue

                    logger.info(f"Command received: {text}")
                    await send_telegram_message(chat_id, "🧠 Processing...")
                    reply = await query_ollama(text)
                    await send_telegram_message(chat_id, reply)
            except Exception:
                logger.exception("Polling error")
                await asyncio.sleep(5)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(poll_telegram())

=== FILE: deploy/DEPLOYMENT.md ===
# ORACLE_CHAT — Oracle Cloud Always Free VM Deployment Guide

## 1. Provision Oracle Always Free VM
1. In Oracle Cloud Console (OCI), create an instance:
   - **Image:** Ubuntu 22.04 or 24.04 (Canonical Ubuntu)
   - **Shape:** `VM.Standard.A1.Flex` (Always Free ARM: up to 4 OCPUs, 24 GB RAM)
   - **Boot Volume:** 50 GB–100 GB (Always Free includes up to 200 GB total)
2. Open port `8080` in OCI Networking:
   - Go to **Networking -> Virtual Cloud Networks -> Your VCN -> Security Lists -> Default Security List**.
   - Click **Add Ingress Rules**:
     - **Source CIDR:** `0.0.0.0/0`
     - **IP Protocol:** `TCP`
     - **Destination Port Range:** `8080`
   - Do **not** open port `11434` publicly; FastAPI communicates with Ollama over `localhost`.

## 2. Clone & Configure on the Oracle VM
SSH into your Oracle VM and run:

    git clone https://github.com/drshariqalikhan/oracle_chat.git
    cd oracle_chat
    cp .env.example .env
    nano .env

Set your preferred defaults in `.env` (for example `OPENROUTER_API_KEY=...` or `LLM_PROVIDER=ollama` + `MODEL_NAME=gemma3:4b`).

## 3. Run the Automated Setup Script

    bash deploy/oracle_setup.sh

To also pull `gemma3:4b` during setup:

    PULL_MODELS=true bash deploy/oracle_setup.sh

## 4. Verify Production Deployment
1. Check systemd services:

    sudo systemctl status oracle-chat
    sudo systemctl status ollama

2. Test endpoints on the VM:

    curl -s http://localhost:8080/health
    curl -s http://localhost:8080/api/providers
    curl -s http://localhost:8080/api/ollama/health

3. Open in your browser:

    http://<YOUR_ORACLE_PUBLIC_IP>:8080

## 5. Updating After Future GitHub Pushes

    cd ~/oracle_chat
    git pull origin main
    source venv/bin/activate
    pip install -r requirements.txt
    sudo systemctl restart oracle-chat

Group=__APP_USER__
WorkingDirectory=__APP_DIR__
EnvironmentFile=__APP_DIR__/.env

Restart=always
RestartSec=5
StandardOutput=journal
StandardError=journal

[Install]

=== FILE: deploy/oracle_setup.sh ===
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

APP_USER="${SUDO_USER:-$USER}"
SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
APP_DIR="$(cd "${SCRIPT_DIR}/.." && pwd)"
APP_PORT="${APP_PORT:-8080}"
PULL_MODELS="${PULL_MODELS:-false}"

echo "=== ORACLE_CHAT Production Setup ==="
echo "User:      ${APP_USER}"
echo "Directory: ${APP_DIR}"
echo "Port:      ${APP_PORT}"

echo "[1/6] Installing Python, Git, zstd, and firewall tools..."
sudo apt-get update -y
sudo DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive apt-get install -y \
    python3 python3-venv python3-pip git curl zstd ufw iptables-persistent

echo "[2/6] Setting up Python virtual environment and dependencies..."
cd "${APP_DIR}"
if [ ! -d "${APP_DIR}/venv" ]; then
    python3 -m venv "${APP_DIR}/venv"
fi
"${APP_DIR}/venv/bin/pip" install --upgrade pip
"${APP_DIR}/venv/bin/pip" install -r "${APP_DIR}/requirements.txt"

echo "[3/6] Ensuring .env configuration exists..."
if [ ! -f "${APP_DIR}/.env" ]; then
    cp "${APP_DIR}/.env.example" "${APP_DIR}/.env"
    echo "Created .env from .env.example — remember to add your OPENROUTER_API_KEY or set LLM_PROVIDER=ollama."
fi

echo "[4/6] Installing and enabling Ollama..."
if ! command -v ollama >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    curl -fsSL https://ollama.com/install.sh | sh
fi
sudo systemctl enable ollama
sudo systemctl restart ollama

if [ "${PULL_MODELS}" = "true" ]; then
    echo "Pulling gemma3:4b (this may take a few minutes)..."
    ollama pull gemma3:4b
fi

echo "[5/6] Configuring OS firewall (iptables + ufw) for SSH (22) and App (${APP_PORT})..."
sudo ufw allow OpenSSH >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
sudo ufw allow "${APP_PORT}/tcp" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
sudo iptables -C INPUT -p tcp --dport "${APP_PORT}" -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null || \
    sudo iptables -I INPUT 6 -m state --state NEW -p tcp --dport "${APP_PORT}" -j ACCEPT
sudo netfilter-persistent save >/dev/null 2>&1 || true

echo "[6/6] Installing and starting systemd service (oracle-chat.service)..."
TMP_UNIT="/tmp/oracle-chat.service"
sed \
    -e "s|__APP_USER__|${APP_USER}|g" \
    -e "s|__APP_DIR__|${APP_DIR}|g" \
    -e "s|__APP_PORT__|${APP_PORT}|g" \
    "${APP_DIR}/deploy/oracle-chat.service" > "${TMP_UNIT}"

sudo cp "${TMP_UNIT}" /etc/systemd/system/oracle-chat.service
rm -f "${TMP_UNIT}"
sudo systemctl daemon-reload
sudo systemctl enable oracle-chat
sudo systemctl restart oracle-chat

echo "=== Setup Complete ==="
echo "Check service status: sudo systemctl status oracle-chat"
echo "Follow live logs:     sudo journalctl -u oracle-chat -f"
echo "Test locally:         curl -s http://localhost:${APP_PORT}/health"

=== END FILE ===
